title: "@duxweb/lumi API"
description: "@duxweb/lumi 的全部公开方法、类型、参数和返回值。"
---

Native json-render integration and data services. 入口：`@duxweb/lumi`、`@duxweb/lumi/catalog`、`@duxweb/lumi/validation-json-schema`。共 528 个公开符号，由 TypeScript 入口自动生成。

## 函数

### aggregateQueryKey

The key of a list's aggregate query.

```typescript
aggregateQueryKey(resource: string, params: unknown, dataProviderName?: string): LumiQueryKey
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `params` | `unknown` | 是 |  |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |

返回 `LumiQueryKey`。

### applyAgentChunk

Folds one stream chunk into the message being built and returns the next
merge state. Unchanged chunks return the same message object, so a renderer
can skip work; changed ones copy only the message and the part that moved.

```typescript
applyAgentChunk(state: AgentMergeState, chunk: AgentChunk, now?: number): AgentMergeState
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `state` | `AgentMergeState` | 是 |  |
| `chunk` | `AgentChunk` | 是 |  |
| `now` | `number` | 否 |  |

`state` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `clientTool` | `((toolName: string) => boolean) \| undefined` | 否 | Tools the front end runs: their calls stay running when the stream ends. |
| `finishReason` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `ids` | `Record<string, string>` | 是 | Text and reasoning ids of this stream mapped to part ids. Servers number blocks per request, so a reply that goes on after a decision may reuse an id the message already has; such a block gets a fresh part id. |
| `message` | `AgentChatMessage` | 是 |  |
| `started` | `Record<string, number>` | 是 | When each reasoning block and tool call started, for its duration. |

返回 `AgentMergeState`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `clientTool` | `((toolName: string) => boolean) \| undefined` | 否 | Tools the front end runs: their calls stay running when the stream ends. |
| `finishReason` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `ids` | `Record<string, string>` | 是 | Text and reasoning ids of this stream mapped to part ids. Servers number blocks per request, so a reply that goes on after a decision may reuse an id the message already has; such a block gets a fresh part id. |
| `message` | `AgentChatMessage` | 是 |  |
| `started` | `Record<string, number>` | 是 | When each reasoning block and tool call started, for its duration. |

### applyOptimisticChange

Applies the change to every cached query of the resource except its
aggregates and audit log. Returns the undo, which puts the saved data back
and refetches the resource, so changes that landed meanwhile — another
write, a refetch — are not lost under the old copy.

```typescript
applyOptimisticChange(client: QueryClient, context: LumiContext, target: { resource: string; dataProviderName?: string; }, change: OptimisticChange): () => void
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `client` | `QueryClient` | 是 |  |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `target` | `{ resource: string; dataProviderName?: string; }` | 是 |  |
| `change` | `OptimisticChange` | 是 |  |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

`target` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |

返回 `() => void`。

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `target` | `T` | 是 |  |
| `sources` | `(object \| undefined)[]` | 是 |  |

返回 `T`。

### composeDefinitions

Merge named definition maps (Catalog components/actions, Registry
components, handlers) into one object. A name defined by more than one
source throws unless it is listed in `override`, in which case the later
source wins. Framework-free: values are copied as-is.

```typescript
composeDefinitions<const T extends readonly object[]>(...args: [...sources: T, options: ComposeDefinitionsOptions]): ComposedDefinitions<T>
composeDefinitions<const T extends readonly object[]>(...sources: T): ComposedDefinitions<T>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `args` | `[...sources: T, options: ComposeDefinitionsOptions]` | 是 |  |

返回 `ComposedDefinitions<T>`。

### connectOptions

Compose a native registry entry for a choice control (Select, MultiSelect,
AutoComplete, Mention, Transfer) whose options may come from a `source`
resource. Without `source` the control keeps its static `options` and needs
no Lumi data services.

```typescript
connectOptions(Component: ComponentType<any>, config?: ConnectOptionsConfig): (context: BaseComponentProps) => ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Component` | `ComponentType<any>` | 是 |  |
| `config` | `ConnectOptionsConfig` | 否 | 创建 Lumi 上下文时使用的 Provider 与运行环境配置。 |

`config` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `events` | `Record<string, string> \| undefined` | 否 | Host callback prop → catalog event it emits, beyond `change`. |
| `mode` | `"single" \| "text" \| "multiple" \| undefined` | 否 | Shape of the bound value: one option value, a list of them, or free text that only takes suggestions. |

返回 `(context: BaseComponentProps) => ReactElement`。

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

返回 `({ props, bindings, emit, }: BaseComponentProps) => JSX.Element`。

### createAgentBridge

The bridge between pages and the agent: pages register what they show, and it answers the UI snapshot and the client tools the agent acts through.

```typescript
createAgentBridge(options?: AgentBridgeOptions): AgentBridge
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `AgentBridgeOptions` | 否 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `approvalMode` | `AgentApprovalMode \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `navigation` | `(() => AgentNavigation \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation used until a host registers its own, e.g. the Lumi router provider. |
| `safeActions` | `readonly string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Actions an event may run without asking; others change data and ask in `ask` mode. |
| `storageKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the approval mode is kept between visits; leave out to keep it in memory. |
| `translate` | `((key: string, fallback: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | Resolves `{ $t }` labels and the approval copy. |

返回 `AgentBridge`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `getApprovalMode` | `() => AgentApprovalMode` | 是 |  |
| `onReference` | `(listener: (items: AgentChatContextItem[]) => void) => () => void` | 是 |  |
| `reference` | `(items: AgentChatContextItem[]) => void` | 是 | Puts records into the conversation, e.g. selected table rows: the open conversation adds them to the next message's context. Kept until one listens. |
| `registerNavigation` | `(navigation: AgentNavigation) => () => void` | 是 |  |
| `registerPage` | `(page: AgentPageHandle) => () => void` | 是 |  |
| `setApprovalMode` | `(mode: AgentApprovalMode) => void` | 是 |  |
| `snapshot` | `() => AgentUiSnapshot` | 是 |  |
| `subscribe` | `(listener: () => void) => () => void` | 是 | Called when the approval mode or the registered pages change. |
| `toolDefinitions` | `() => AgentToolDefinition[]` | 是 |  |
| `tools` | `() => Record<string, AgentClientTool>` | 是 |  |

### createAgentSession

A conversation with an agent endpoint: the messages, whether a reply is
under way, and the reader's actions on it. Framework-free; the React hook
and the JSON AgentChatSession component share one instance per conversation.

```typescript
createAgentSession(options: AgentChatSessionOptions): AgentChatSession
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `AgentChatSessionOptions` | 是 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `approvalMode` | `(() => AgentApprovalMode) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `clientTools` | `(() => Record<string, AgentClientTool>) \| undefined` | 否 | Tools the front end runs, by name. A call to one stays running when its reply ends; the session runs it (asking first in `ask` mode when the tool wants approval), records the output and lets the reply go on. |
| `generateId` | `(() => string) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `id` | `string \| null \| undefined` | 否 | 记录主键。 |
| `messages` | `AgentChatMessage[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `now` | `(() => number) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `onData` | `((chunk: AgentChunk) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Every `data-*` chunk as it arrives, transient ones included. |
| `onError` | `((error: Error) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | 请求失败后的回调。 |
| `onFinish` | `((event: AgentFinishEvent) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `transport` | `AgentTransport` | 是 |  |

返回 `AgentChatSession`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `answer` | `(questionId: string, answer: string \| string[]) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 |  |
| `approve` | `(approvalId: string, approved: boolean, reason?: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Decides an approval; the reply goes on once nothing in the message waits for the reader. |
| `edit` | `(messageId: string, text: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Rewrites a user message and asks again from there; later messages are dropped. |
| `getSnapshot` | `() => AgentChatSnapshot` | 是 |  |
| `reset` | `(next?: { id?: string \| null; messages?: AgentChatMessage[]; }) => void` | 是 | Switches to another conversation (or a new one), stopping any reply under way. |
| `retry` | `() => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Asks again for the answer to the last user message. |
| `send` | `(input: AgentSendInput) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Resolves false without sending while a reply is under way or the message is empty. |
| `skip` | `(questionId: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 |  |
| `stop` | `() => void` | 是 |  |
| `subscribe` | `(listener: () => void) => () => void` | 是 |  |

### createDataAuditLogProvider

A log kept as one more resource of the DataProvider: events are created in
it, and listed with `resource` and `recordId` filters, newest first. A
write to one record also carries that record as `recordId`, so its history
is one plain filter; bulk writes keep their `ids` only.

```typescript
createDataAuditLogProvider({ dataProvider: source, resource }: DataAuditLogProviderOptions): AuditLogProvider
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `__0` | `DataAuditLogProviderOptions` | 是 |  |

`__0` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProvider` | `DataProvider \| (() => DataProvider)` | 是 | The provider holding the log, or a function returning it when each call is made. |
| `resource` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The log's own resource on the backend. |

返回 `AuditLogProvider`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `create` | `((event: AuditLogEvent) => Promise<unknown> \| void) \| undefined` | 否 | Records one accepted write. |
| `get` | `((params: AuditLogListParams) => Promise<{ data: AuditLogEntry[]; total?: number; }>) \| undefined` | 否 | Lists the entries of a resource, or of one record with `id`. |
| `update` | `((params: { id: ID; name: string; }) => Promise<unknown>) \| undefined` | 否 | Names an entry, e.g. with a release note. |

### createHttpAgentTransport

Posts the conversation as JSON and reads the reply as an AI SDK UI message
stream. The body follows the AI SDK chat request: `id`, `messages`,
`trigger` and, for regeneration and decisions, `messageId`.

```typescript
createHttpAgentTransport(options: HttpAgentTransportOptions): AgentTransport
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `HttpAgentTransportOptions` | 是 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `body` | `Record<string, unknown> \| (() => Record<string, unknown>) \| undefined` | 否 | Extra fields sent with every request, e.g. the model. |
| `credentials` | `RequestCredentials \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `fetch` | `((input: RequestInfo \| URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `headers` | `AgentHeaders \| undefined` | 否 | Extra headers; the auth provider's credentials come through `http`. |
| `http` | `HttpClient \| undefined` | 否 | Sends through this client, so the auth provider signs the request and a 401 refreshes once; without one the request goes out unsigned. |
| `prepareBody` | `((request: AgentRequest, body: Record<string, unknown>) => Record<string, unknown>) \| undefined` | 否 | Replaces the default request body `{ id, messages, trigger, messageId, ...body }`, e.g. to send only the last message to a server that keeps the history. |
| `url` | `string \| (() => string)` | 是 | The chat endpoint, e.g. `/agent/chat`; a function is read on every request. |

返回 `AgentTransport`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `send` | `(request: AgentRequest) => Promise<AsyncIterable<AgentChunk>>` | 是 |  |

### createHttpClient

The one request path of Lumi. Data providers, uploads, the agent, the live
stream and the auth provider's own sign-in requests all go through a client:
it asks the auth for the request's credentials (`authorizeRequest`), adds
the locale and tenant, encodes JSON, refreshes once on a 401 and turns a
non-2xx answer into a `DataProviderError`. The data layer passes a signed
client's failures to the auth's `onError`; an unsigned one's to the caller.

All of that is for the client's own backend: `baseUrl`, the page's site and
`trustedOrigins`. A full URL to anywhere else is sent plain, so a page that
names a foreign address never carries the reader's credentials there.

```typescript
createHttpClient(options?: HttpClientOptions): HttpClient
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `HttpClientOptions` | 否 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `auth` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | `false` sends no app credentials and leaves failures to the caller: a 401 from such a backend does not sign the reader out. Its own key, if any, goes in `headers`. Defaults to `true`. |
| `authProvider` | `RequestSigner \| (() => RequestSigner \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | The app's auth provider (or just its signing part); a function is read on every request. |
| `baseUrl` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Root that relative paths join onto; absolute URLs pass through. |
| `fetcher` | `((input: RequestInfo \| URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>) \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to the global fetch, read when a request starts. |
| `getLocale` | `(() => string \| null \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | Current UI locale, sent as `Accept-Language`. |
| `getTenant` | `(() => string \| null \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | Current tenant, sent as `tenantHeader`. |
| `headers` | `HttpHeaders \| undefined` | 否 | Headers on every request, below the per-request and auth ones; a function is read on every request. |
| `onRequest` | `((request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => HttpOutgoingRequest \| void \| Promise<HttpOutgoingRequest \| void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Last look at a request before it leaves, after the credentials, locale, tenant and JSON encoding: add or rewrite headers, sign the method, URL and body. Runs again for the resend after a 401 refresh. Change the request in place or return a new one. |
| `onResponse` | `((response: Response, request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => Response \| void \| Promise<Response \| void>) \| undefined` | 否 | First look at the final answer, whatever its status: check its signature, decrypt it, or reshape it. Return another Response to replace it; throw to fail the request. |
| `parseError` | `((body: unknown, response: Response) => HttpErrorDetails) \| undefined` | 否 | Reads this backend's error body (parsed JSON, or `{}` when it is not JSON) into the one `DataProviderError` every caller handles. Defaults to `parseLumiError`. |
| `tenantHeader` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to `X-Tenant`. |
| `trustedOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Other sites that get the same treatment as `baseUrl` and the page, e.g. `["https://files.example.com"]`. A request to any other site goes out plain: without the reader's credentials, the tenant, the default headers or the hooks, and its 401 does not sign the reader out. |

返回 `HttpClient`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `baseUrl` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `download` | `(path: string, options?: HttpRequestOptions) => Promise<Response>` | 是 | The same request, for an answer that is a file: the response itself on 2xx, its body unread so the caller can follow the stream, else a `DataProviderError`. |
| `extend` | `(options: HttpClientExtendOptions) => HttpClient` | 是 |  |
| `fetch` | `(path: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>` | 是 | A signed fetch: on a 401 the auth's `refresh` runs once (shared by every request waiting on it) and the request is sent again. The response is returned as it is, whatever its status. |
| `getAuthProvider` | `() => RequestSigner \| undefined` | 是 | The provider signing these requests, if any. |
| `own` | `<E>(error: E) => E` | 是 | Marks a failure as this client's, so only a signed client's reaches the auth's `onError`. |
| `prepare` | `(request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => Promise<HttpPreparedRequest>` | 是 | The request as this client sends it: credentials, locale, tenant, then `onRequest`. For transports `fetch` does not cover, such as an XHR upload, which hands the answer to `receive`. |
| `receive` | `(response: Response, request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => Promise<Response>` | 是 | Runs `onResponse` on an answer to a prepared request. |
| `request` | `<T = any>(path: string, options?: HttpRequestOptions) => Promise<T>` | 是 | A signed JSON request; the parsed body on 2xx, else a `DataProviderError`. |
| `signed` | `boolean` | 是 | Whether the app's auth signs these requests and handles their failures. |
| `url` | `(path: string) => string` | 是 | The path joined onto `baseUrl`. |

### createI18nProvider

An i18n provider over translation tables, layered on Lumi's own texts, with locale-aware number, date, currency and datetime formats.

```typescript
createI18nProvider(options: CreateI18nProviderOptions): I18nProvider
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `CreateI18nProviderOptions` | 是 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `direction` | `LumiDirection \| ((locale: string) => LumiDirection) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `fallbackLocale` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `format` | `I18nFormatConfig \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `locale` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `translations` | `I18nTranslations \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `I18nProvider`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `changeLocale` | `(locale: string, params?: unknown) => Promise<void> \| void` | 是 | Switches the locale. |
| `formatCurrency` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An amount in the configured currency and unit. |
| `formatDate` | `((value: I18nDateValue, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A date in the locale's form. |
| `formatDateTime` | `((value: string, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An ISO 8601 UTC string shown in the configured time zone. |
| `formatNumber` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A number in the locale's form; components use `Intl` without it. |
| `formatRelativeTime` | `((value: number, unit: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatUnit, options?: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A relative time such as "3 days ago". |
| `getDirection` | `(() => LumiDirection) \| undefined` | 否 | The current locale's text direction. |
| `getFallbackLocale` | `(() => string \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | The locale a missing translation falls back to, when there is one. |
| `getFormatConfig` | `(() => I18nFormatConfig) \| undefined` | 否 | The currency and time zone settings. |
| `getLocale` | `() => string` | 是 | The current locale, e.g. `zh-CN`. |
| `subscribe` | `((listener: () => void) => () => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Notifies when the locale changes; with it `$t` and component text follow the switch. |
| `translate` | `(key: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, defaultMessage?: string) => string` | 是 | The text for `key` in the current locale, with `{name}` placeholders filled from `params`; `defaultMessage`, else the key, when there is none. |

### createJsonLoader

A loader of JSON documents, by URL or as an inline value: fetches, unpacks a packed answer, validates, and caches with ETag revalidation; concurrent loads of one URL share a request.

```typescript
createJsonLoader<T>(validate: (value: unknown) => DocumentValidation, options?: CreateLumiLoaderOptions): LumiJsonLoader<T>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `validate` | `(value: unknown) => DocumentValidation` | 是 |  |
| `options` | `CreateLumiLoaderOptions` | 否 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `baseURL` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `cacheTime` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `fetcher` | `((input: RequestInfo \| URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `headers` | `LumiRequestHeaders \| (() => LumiRequestHeaders \| Promise<LumiRequestHeaders>) \| undefined` | 否 | 请求头。 |
| `packKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Key of packed answers, when the server masks them with one. A server may answer `{ "packed": "…" }` (see `packDocument`) instead of the document, so its text does not show in the network panel; the loader unpacks it before validating and caching. Readable JSON loads as before. |
| `timeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | 超时毫秒数。 |
| `trustedOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `LumiJsonLoader<T>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `invalidate` | `(url?: string, cacheKey?: string) => void` | 是 |  |
| `load` | `(input: string \| T, options?: LumiLoadOptions) => Promise<T>` | 是 |  |
| `peek` | `(url: string, cacheKey?: string) => T \| undefined` | 是 |  |

### createLumi

Framework-agnostic context factory: holds providers with no reactivity.
Framework entrypoints layer reactivity and components on top.

```typescript
createLumi(config?: LumiConfig): LumiContext
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `config` | `LumiConfig` | 否 | 创建 Lumi 上下文时使用的 Provider 与运行环境配置。 |

`config` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | 权限判断。 |
| `agent` | `AgentConfig \| undefined` | 否 | Agent chat requests: the default endpoint, the data source and extra headers. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Records each accepted write and lists the record history; optional. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The app's one sign-in session; it also signs every request `http` sends. |
| `dataProvider` | `DataProvider \| Record<string, DataProvider> \| undefined` | 否 | Pass one for a single data source; pass \{ default: ..., other: ... \} for multiple |
| `env` | `Record<string, string \| number \| boolean \| null> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `http` | `HttpClient \| undefined` | 否 | The client uploads and the agent send through when their data provider has none. Defaults to one signed by `authProvider` that sends the locale and tenant; data providers built on it share those credentials. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | 翻译、切换语言和格式化。 |
| `liveMode` | `LiveMode \| undefined` | 否 | The default for every query; `auto` when a liveProvider is set. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Pushes backend changes; with it, queries refetch what an event touches. |
| `mutationMode` | `MutationMode \| undefined` | 否 | The default for updates and deletes; `pessimistic` waits for the server. Forms always wait. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws `notify` notices; without one the action is a no-op. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | 与框架无关的导航。 |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The tenant (workspace) requests belong to; queries refetch when it changes. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws `toast` feedback and automatic error reports; without one they are no-ops. |
| `undoableTimeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Milliseconds an undoable write waits for Undo; defaults to 5000. |
| `upload` | `UploadOptions \| undefined` | 否 | 全局上传地址、请求头和允许的目标来源。 |
| `validator` | `Validator \| undefined` | 否 | 统一的数据校验器，可接 AJV、Zod、Valibot 或自定义实现。 |

返回 `LumiContext`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

返回 `Record<string, ActionHandler>`。

### createOverlayController

The store of open overlays behind `useOverlay`: opens them, runs their close guards and resolves their results.

```typescript
createOverlayController(): OverlayController
```

返回 `OverlayController`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dispose` | `() => void` | 是 | Closes every overlay and drops the listeners. |
| `entries` | `OverlayEntry[]` | 是 | The open overlays, the newest last. |
| `getSnapshot` | `() => OverlayEntry[]` | 是 | The open overlays as an immutable list, for `useSyncExternalStore`. |
| `open` | `(options: OverlayOptions) => OverlayHandle` | 是 | Opens an overlay; the handle closes it and carries its `result`. |
| `registerCloseGuard` | `(id: number, guard: OverlayCloseGuard) => () => void` | 是 | Adds a check that may refuse a close; returns the function removing it. |
| `requestClose` | `(id: number, request?: { reason?: OverlayCloseReason; value?: unknown; }) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Closes an overlay once its close guards agree; resolves whether it closed. |
| `subscribe` | `(listener: () => void) => () => void` | 是 | Notifies when the open overlays change. |

The access check of the simple convention, on its own for providers that
keep their own permission list: `can(permissions, { resource, action })`.


| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `SimplePermissionFormat` | 否 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `format` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | With `{resource}`, `{action}` and `{separator}`; `{action}{separator}{resource}` reads `delete:system:users`. |
| `separator` | `":" \| "." \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to `:`. |

返回 `(permissions: readonly string[], params: { resource: string; action: string; }) => boolean`。

### createSimpleAuthProvider

The session conventions a JSON backend most often speaks. Bearer mode:
login returns a token plus the user and permission list, `me` restores
them. Cookie mode: the server keeps the session in a cookie and `me` says
who is signed in. Permissions read as `resource:action` (`orders:*`, `*:*`).
One session feeds both the auth and access providers, so a JSON-configured
base needs no code for it; its own requests go through an HttpClient it
signs itself. Session requests (login, logout, refresh) carry whatever
session there is and never trigger a refresh.

```typescript
createSimpleAuthProvider(options: SimpleAuthProviderOptions): SimpleAuth
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `SimpleAuthProviderOptions` | 是 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `apiUrl` | `string` | 是 | API root the endpoints are relative to. |
| `csrf` | `SimpleAuthCsrf \| undefined` | 否 | Cookie mode: the anti-forgery cookie echoed in a header on writes. |
| `endpoints` | `SimpleAuthEndpoints \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `http` | `HttpClient \| undefined` | 否 | The client its own requests go through (for the locale, tenant or a test fetcher); one is created otherwise. |
| `loginPath` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where a signed-out reader is sent. |
| `mode` | `"bearer" \| "cookie" \| undefined` | 否 | `bearer` (default) keeps the token login returns and sends it as `Authorization: Bearer …`. `cookie` keeps nothing: the server's session cookie travels with `credentials: 'include'` and `me` tells who is in. |
| `permission` | `SimplePermissionFormat \| undefined` | 否 | How a `{ resource, action }` check reads the permission strings login returns. |
| `readCookie` | `((name: string) => string \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | Cookie mode: where `csrf.cookie` is read; defaults to `document.cookie`. |
| `storage` | `TokenStorage \| undefined` | 否 | Bearer mode: defaults to `globalThis.localStorage`; pass one for SSR or tests. |
| `tokenKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Bearer mode: storage key for the token. |

返回 `SimpleAuth`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider` | 是 | 权限判断。 |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider` | 是 | 登录、退出、会话检查和读取身份。 |
| `getSession` | `() => SimpleAuthSession` | 是 |  |

### createSimpleDataProvider

Default REST DataProvider — follows the Lumi REST conventions (framework-agnostic, fetch-based):
- List    GET    \{apiUrl\}/\{resource\}?page&pageSize&status=paid&total[gte]=100&sort=-createdAt,name
- Detail  GET    \{apiUrl\}/\{resource\}/\{id\}
- Create  POST   \{apiUrl\}/\{resource\} (there is no bulk create: hooks and imports send one request per record)
- Update  PUT    \{apiUrl\}/\{resource\}/\{id\}
- Delete  DELETE \{apiUrl\}/\{resource\}/\{id\}
- Credentials, locale and tenant come from `http`
- Response  \{ message?, data, meta:\{ total \} \}, data is auto-unwrapped
- Errors    non-2xx \{ message, code?, errors?: [\{ path, message, code? \}] \}

```typescript
createSimpleDataProvider(options: SimpleDataProviderOptions): DataProvider
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `SimpleDataProviderOptions` | 是 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `apiUrl` | `string` | 是 | API root URL, e.g. https://api.example.com |
| `http` | `HttpClient \| undefined` | 否 | The client requests go through, carrying the auth provider's credentials, the locale and the tenant. Without one requests carry none of them. |
| `onRequest` | `((request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => HttpOutgoingRequest \| void \| Promise<HttpOutgoingRequest \| void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Changes every request to this backend just before it leaves: extra or rewritten headers, a signature over the method, URL and body. See `HttpClientOptions.onRequest`. |
| `onResponse` | `((response: Response, request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => Response \| void \| Promise<Response \| void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Checks or rewrites every answer from this backend, e.g. verifies its signature. |
| `parseError` | `((body: unknown, response: Response) => HttpErrorDetails) \| undefined` | 否 | Reads a failed answer's body into `{ message, code?, errors?: [{ path, message }] }`; defaults to the Lumi convention. Field errors appear under the form's fields. |
| `unwrap` | `((body: any) => { data: any; total?: number; raw?: unknown; }) \| undefined` | 否 | Custom response unwrapping; defaults to the Lumi convention: data = body.data ?? body, total = body.meta.total |

返回 `DataProvider`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `create` | `<T = any, V = any>(params: CreateParams<V>) => Promise<SingleResult<T>>` | 是 | Creates a record from `variables` and answers the saved one. |
| `createMany` | `(<T = any, V = any>(params: CreateManyParams<V>) => Promise<ManyResult<T>>) \| undefined` | 否 | Creates several records in one request; without it they are created one by one. |
| `custom` | `(<T = any>(params: CustomParams) => Promise<SingleResult<T>>) \| undefined` | 否 | A request outside the resource conventions; the `request` action, `useCustom` and `useCustomMutation` need it. |
| `deleteMany` | `(<T = any, V = any>(params: DeleteManyParams<V>) => Promise<ManyResult<T>>) \| undefined` | 否 | Deletes several records; without it they are deleted one by one. |
| `deleteOne` | `<T = any, V = any>(params: DeleteParams<V>) => Promise<SingleResult<T>>` | 是 | Deletes the record `id`. |
| `getApiUrl` | `(() => string) \| undefined` | 否 | The API root, for callers that build full addresses such as uploads. |
| `getList` | `<T = any>(params: GetListParams) => Promise<GetListResult<T>>` | 是 | One page of a resource, filtered and sorted, or all of it with `pagination: false`; `total` counts every match. |
| `getMany` | `(<T = any>(params: GetManyParams) => Promise<ManyResult<T>>) \| undefined` | 否 | Several records by id in one request; without it each id is read with `getOne`. |
| `getOne` | `<T = any>(params: GetOneParams) => Promise<SingleResult<T>>` | 是 | One record by id. |
| `http` | `HttpClient \| undefined` | 否 | The client it sends through; uploads and the agent reuse its credentials. |
| `update` | `<T = any, V = any>(params: UpdateParams<V>) => Promise<SingleResult<T>>` | 是 | Updates the record `id` with `variables` and answers the saved one. |
| `updateMany` | `(<T = any, V = any>(params: UpdateManyParams<V>) => Promise<ManyResult<T>>) \| undefined` | 否 | Applies the same `variables` to several records; without it they are updated one by one. |

### createSpecLoader

A loader of page Specs, validated against the Catalog.

```typescript
createSpecLoader(catalog: Catalog, options?: CreateLumiLoaderOptions & DocumentValidationOptions): LumiJsonLoader<Spec>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `catalog` | `Catalog<SchemaDefinition<SchemaType<string, unknown>, SchemaType<string, unknown>>, unknown>` | 是 |  |
| `options` | `(CreateLumiLoaderOptions & { maxElements?: number; maxDepth?: number; directives?: readonly DirectiveDefinition[]; }) \| undefined` | 否 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `baseURL` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `cacheTime` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `directives` | `readonly DirectiveDefinition<z.ZodType<unknown, unknown, z.core.$ZodTypeInternals<unknown, unknown>>>[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `fetcher` | `((input: RequestInfo \| URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `headers` | `LumiRequestHeaders \| (() => LumiRequestHeaders \| Promise<LumiRequestHeaders>) \| undefined` | 否 | 请求头。 |
| `maxDepth` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `maxElements` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `packKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Key of packed answers, when the server masks them with one. A server may answer `{ "packed": "…" }` (see `packDocument`) instead of the document, so its text does not show in the network panel; the loader unpacks it before validating and caching. Readable JSON loads as before. |
| `timeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | 超时毫秒数。 |
| `trustedOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `LumiJsonLoader<Spec>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `invalidate` | `(url?: string, cacheKey?: string) => void` | 是 |  |
| `load` | `(input: string \| Spec, options?: LumiLoadOptions) => Promise<Spec>` | 是 |  |
| `peek` | `(url: string, cacheKey?: string) => Spec \| undefined` | 是 |  |

A server may answer `{ "packed": "…" }` (see `packDocument`) instead of the document, so its text does not show in the network panel; the loader unpacks it before validating and caching. ### createSseLiveProvider

Realtime over one server-sent event stream read with fetch, so it carries
the same headers as data requests (EventSource cannot). The stream opens
with the first subscription, closes with the last, and reconnects with
backoff after a drop.

```typescript
createSseLiveProvider(options: SseLiveProviderOptions): SseLiveProvider
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `SseLiveProviderOptions` | 是 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `credentials` | `RequestCredentials \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `fetcher` | `((input: RequestInfo \| URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `headers` | `(() => Record<string, string>) \| undefined` | 否 | Extra headers for each connection, e.g. the token and the tenant; read again on every reconnect. |
| `http` | `HttpClient \| undefined` | 否 | Opens and posts through this client, so the auth provider signs each connection (read again on every reconnect) with the locale and tenant. |
| `publishUrl` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where `publish` posts an event; without it the provider only listens. |
| `retryDelay` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | First wait before reconnecting, doubled up to 30 seconds. |
| `url` | `string` | 是 | The event stream endpoint; it sends `data: { channel, type, payload }` frames. |

返回 `SseLiveProvider`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `close` | `() => void` | 是 |  |
| `publish` | `((event: LiveEvent) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Sends an event to the backend, for providers that can. |
| `reconnect` | `() => void` | 是 | Closes and opens the stream again, e.g. once the tenant or the token changed. |
| `subscribe` | `(params: LiveSubscribeParams) => () => void` | 是 | Returns the function that ends the subscription. |

### createTenantProvider

A tenant provider kept in memory, or in local storage with `storageKey`. A
stored tenant that is no longer among `options` is ignored.

```typescript
createTenantProvider(options?: CreateTenantProviderOptions): TenantProvider
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `CreateTenantProviderOptions` | 否 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `initial` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The tenant before the reader picks one; defaults to the first option. |
| `options` | `readonly TenantOption[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `storageKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Keeps the choice between visits under this local storage key. |

返回 `TenantProvider`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `get` | `() => string \| undefined` | 是 | The current tenant's value, or undefined before one is chosen. |
| `options` | `(() => readonly TenantOption[]) \| undefined` | 否 | The tenants the reader may switch between, when the provider knows them. |
| `set` | `(value: string) => void` | 是 | Switches to another tenant. |
| `subscribe` | `(listener: () => void) => () => void` | 是 | Notifies when the tenant changes; Lumi drops its cached data on it. |

### customQueryKey

The key of a custom request, by every one of its params.

```typescript
customQueryKey(params: CustomQueryParams, dataProviderName?: string): LumiQueryKey
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `params` | `CustomQueryParams` | 是 |  |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |

`params` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `filters` | `Filter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 筛选条件数组。 |
| `headers` | `Record<string, string> \| undefined` | 否 | 请求头。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `method` | `"GET" \| "POST" \| "PUT" \| "PATCH" \| "DELETE" \| undefined` | 否 | `GET` by default. |
| `payload` | `unknown` | 否 | The request body; not allowed with `GET`. |
| `query` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Query parameters. |
| `sorters` | `Sorter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 排序条件数组。 |
| `url` | `string` | 是 | The endpoint, relative to the API root. |

返回 `LumiQueryKey`。

### dataProviderHttp

The client that reaches a data provider's backend: the provider's own, else
the app's with that backend's API root trusted, so a hand-written provider
on another origin still gets the reader's credentials for its uploads,
exports and agent requests.

```typescript
dataProviderHttp(context: LumiContext, dataProviderName?: string): HttpClient
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

返回 `HttpClient`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `baseUrl` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `download` | `(path: string, options?: HttpRequestOptions) => Promise<Response>` | 是 | The same request, for an answer that is a file: the response itself on 2xx, its body unread so the caller can follow the stream, else a `DataProviderError`. |
| `extend` | `(options: HttpClientExtendOptions) => HttpClient` | 是 |  |
| `fetch` | `(path: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>` | 是 | A signed fetch: on a 401 the auth's `refresh` runs once (shared by every request waiting on it) and the request is sent again. The response is returned as it is, whatever its status. |
| `getAuthProvider` | `() => RequestSigner \| undefined` | 是 | The provider signing these requests, if any. |
| `own` | `<E>(error: E) => E` | 是 | Marks a failure as this client's, so only a signed client's reaches the auth's `onError`. |
| `prepare` | `(request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => Promise<HttpPreparedRequest>` | 是 | The request as this client sends it: credentials, locale, tenant, then `onRequest`. For transports `fetch` does not cover, such as an XHR upload, which hands the answer to `receive`. |
| `receive` | `(response: Response, request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => Promise<Response>` | 是 | Runs `onResponse` on an answer to a prepared request. |
| `request` | `<T = any>(path: string, options?: HttpRequestOptions) => Promise<T>` | 是 | A signed JSON request; the parsed body on 2xx, else a `DataProviderError`. |
| `signed` | `boolean` | 是 | Whether the app's auth signs these requests and handles their failures. |
| `url` | `(path: string) => string` | 是 | The path joined onto `baseUrl`. |

| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |

### downloadExport

Asks the backend for the export file and follows its stream. `total` is the
`Content-Length` when the server sends one; `loaded` counts bytes.

```typescript
downloadExport(context: LumiContext, client: QueryClient | undefined, input: RunExportOptions & { url: string; }, save: TransferHost["save"]): Promise<TransferDownload>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `client` | `QueryClient \| undefined` | 是 |  |
| `input` | `RunExportOptions & { url: string; }` | 是 |  |
| `save` | `(file: TransferFile \| TransferDownload) => void` | 是 |  |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `columns` | `readonly TransferColumn[] \| undefined` | 否 | The columns of the file, in order; every field of the records when left out. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `filename` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Name of the saved file without its extension; the resource name by default. |
| `filters` | `Filter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 筛选条件数组。 |
| `format` | `(string & {}) \| TransferFormat \| undefined` | 否 | `csv` or `json` for a file written here; with `url`, any name the backend knows (`xlsx`…). |
| `maxItems` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Stops after this many records, so an export cannot run away. |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `method` | `"GET" \| "POST" \| undefined` | 否 | `POST` (default) sends the values as JSON; `GET` as the query, `filters`, `sorters` and `columns` as JSON text. |
| `onProgress` | `((progress: { loaded: number; total?: number; }) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `pageSize` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Records asked for per request. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | 用于取消请求的 AbortSignal。 |
| `sorters` | `Sorter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 排序条件数组。 |
| `url` | `string` | 是 | The backend builds the file: this endpoint (relative to the API root) is sent `{ filters, sorters, columns, format, ...meta }` and answers the file as a stream, in any format. Without it the records are loaded page by page and the file is written in the browser. |

返回 `Promise<TransferDownload>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `content` | `Blob` | 是 |  |
| `name` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `type` | `string` | 是 |  |

### executeAccountAction

Runs an account flow that leaves the session as it is, such as requesting or setting a password.

```typescript
executeAccountAction(context: LumiContext, operation: AuthAccountAction, params?: unknown): Promise<AuthActionResponse>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `operation` | `AuthAccountAction` | 是 |  |
| `params` | `unknown` | 否 |  |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

返回 `Promise<AuthActionResponse>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `error` | `Error \| undefined` | 否 | Why it failed. |
| `redirectTo` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the reader goes next. |
| `success` | `boolean` | 是 | `false` keeps the current session; the sign-in page shows `error`. |

| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |

| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

返回 `Promise<AuthActionResponse>`。

### executeMutation

Runs a write, records it in the audit log, then refetches the resource's queries; a failed refetch is reported as `refreshError` on the result, never as a failed write.

```typescript
executeMutation<T extends object>(context: LumiContext, client: QueryClient, request: () => Promise<T>, target: { resource?: string; dataProviderName?: string; deletedIds?: readonly ID[]; audit?: Omit<AuditLogEvent, "resource">; }): Promise<MutationResult<T>>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `client` | `QueryClient` | 是 |  |
| `request` | `() => Promise<T>` | 是 |  |
| `target` | `{ resource?: string; dataProviderName?: string; deletedIds?: readonly ID[]; audit?: Omit<AuditLogEvent, "resource">; }` | 是 |  |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

`target` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `audit` | `Omit<AuditLogEvent, "resource"> \| undefined` | 否 | Recorded through the audit log provider once the server accepts the write. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `deletedIds` | `readonly ID[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `resource` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |

返回 `Promise<MutationResult<T>>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `refreshError` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

Runs a data request and hands a failure to the auth provider's `onError`, which may sign the reader out; a failure of an unsigned backend (`auth: false`) goes to the caller alone.

| `client` | `QueryClient \| undefined` | 否 |  |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

返回 `Promise<T>`。

### executeWrite

The one write path the hooks and the JSON handlers share: it decides the
mutation mode, the optimistic change, the records to drop from the cache
and the audit event, then sends the request through executeMutation.

```typescript
executeWrite<T extends object>(context: LumiContext, client: QueryClient, options: WriteOptions<T>): Promise<MutationResult<T>>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `client` | `QueryClient` | 是 |  |
| `options` | `WriteOptions<T>` | 是 |  |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `ids` | `readonly ID[] \| undefined` | 否 | The records written; known for updates and deletes. |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `mutationMode` | `MutationMode \| undefined` | 否 | Falls back to `config.mutationMode`; only updates and deletes with ids use it. |
| `operation` | `WriteOperation` | 是 |  |
| `request` | `() => Promise<T>` | 是 | Sends the write to the DataProvider. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `undoableTimeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `values` | `unknown` | 否 | The values sent, recorded in the audit log and patched into the cache. |

返回 `Promise<MutationResult<T>>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `refreshError` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

Builds the export file of the records, CSV or JSON, with its name and MIME type.


| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `rows` | `readonly Record<string, unknown>[]` | 是 |  |
| `options` | `{ filename?: string; format?: TransferFormat; columns?: readonly TransferColumn[]; }` | 是 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `columns` | `readonly TransferColumn[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `filename` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `format` | `TransferFormat \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `TransferFile`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |

| `content` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `name` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `type` | `string` | 是 |  |

### extendI18nProvider

Adds translations beneath an existing i18n provider: its own text wins and these fill what it lacks. Without a provider, one is created from them.

```typescript
extendI18nProvider(provider: I18nProvider | undefined, translations: I18nTranslations, options?: ExtendI18nProviderOptions): I18nProvider
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `provider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 是 |  |
| `translations` | `I18nTranslations` | 是 |  |
| `options` | `ExtendI18nProviderOptions` | 否 |  |

`provider` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `changeLocale` | `(locale: string, params?: unknown) => Promise<void> \| void` | 是 | Switches the locale. |
| `formatCurrency` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An amount in the configured currency and unit. |
| `formatDate` | `((value: I18nDateValue, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A date in the locale's form. |
| `formatDateTime` | `((value: string, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An ISO 8601 UTC string shown in the configured time zone. |
| `formatNumber` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A number in the locale's form; components use `Intl` without it. |
| `formatRelativeTime` | `((value: number, unit: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatUnit, options?: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A relative time such as "3 days ago". |
| `getDirection` | `(() => LumiDirection) \| undefined` | 否 | The current locale's text direction. |
| `getFallbackLocale` | `(() => string \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | The locale a missing translation falls back to, when there is one. |
| `getFormatConfig` | `(() => I18nFormatConfig) \| undefined` | 否 | The currency and time zone settings. |
| `getLocale` | `() => string` | 是 | The current locale, e.g. `zh-CN`. |
| `subscribe` | `((listener: () => void) => () => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Notifies when the locale changes; with it `$t` and component text follow the switch. |
| `translate` | `(key: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, defaultMessage?: string) => string` | 是 | The text for `key` in the current locale, with `{name}` placeholders filled from `params`; `defaultMessage`, else the key, when there is none. |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `direction` | `LumiDirection \| ((locale: string) => LumiDirection) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `fallbackLocale` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `format` | `I18nFormatConfig \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `locale` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `I18nProvider`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `changeLocale` | `(locale: string, params?: unknown) => Promise<void> \| void` | 是 | Switches the locale. |
| `formatCurrency` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An amount in the configured currency and unit. |
| `formatDate` | `((value: I18nDateValue, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A date in the locale's form. |
| `formatDateTime` | `((value: string, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An ISO 8601 UTC string shown in the configured time zone. |
| `formatNumber` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A number in the locale's form; components use `Intl` without it. |
| `formatRelativeTime` | `((value: number, unit: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatUnit, options?: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A relative time such as "3 days ago". |
| `getDirection` | `(() => LumiDirection) \| undefined` | 否 | The current locale's text direction. |
| `getFallbackLocale` | `(() => string \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | The locale a missing translation falls back to, when there is one. |
| `getFormatConfig` | `(() => I18nFormatConfig) \| undefined` | 否 | The currency and time zone settings. |
| `getLocale` | `() => string` | 是 | The current locale, e.g. `zh-CN`. |
| `subscribe` | `((listener: () => void) => () => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Notifies when the locale changes; with it `$t` and component text follow the switch. |
| `translate` | `(key: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, defaultMessage?: string) => string` | 是 | The text for `key` in the current locale, with `{name}` placeholders filled from `params`; `defaultMessage`, else the key, when there is none. |

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `formatRelativeTime` | `((value: number, unit: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatUnit, options?: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A relative time such as "3 days ago". |

| `getFormatConfig` | `(() => I18nFormatConfig) \| undefined` | 否 | The currency and time zone settings. |
| `getLocale` | `() => string` | 是 | The current locale, e.g. `zh-CN`. |
| `subscribe` | `((listener: () => void) => () => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Notifies when the locale changes; with it `$t` and component text follow the switch. |
| `translate` | `(key: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, defaultMessage?: string) => string` | 是 | The text for `key` in the current locale, with `{name}` placeholders filled from `params`; `defaultMessage`, else the key, when there is none. |

### fetchAllRecords

Every record matching the filters, page after page through `getList`.

```typescript
fetchAllRecords<T = Record<string, unknown>>(context: LumiContext, client: QueryClient | undefined, options: FetchAllOptions): Promise<T[]>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `client` | `QueryClient \| undefined` | 是 |  |
| `options` | `FetchAllOptions` | 是 |  |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `filters` | `Filter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 筛选条件数组。 |
| `maxItems` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Stops after this many records, so an export cannot run away. |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `onProgress` | `((progress: { loaded: number; total?: number; }) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `pageSize` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Records asked for per request. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | 用于取消请求的 AbortSignal。 |
| `sorters` | `Sorter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 排序条件数组。 |

返回 `Promise<T[]>`。

### findAgentSession

The agent session an `AgentSession` element mounted at a state path; throws when there is none.

```typescript
findAgentSession(owner: object, path: string): AgentChatSession
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `owner` | `object` | 是 |  |
| `path` | `string` | 是 |  |

返回 `AgentChatSession`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `answer` | `(questionId: string, answer: string \| string[]) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 |  |
| `approve` | `(approvalId: string, approved: boolean, reason?: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Decides an approval; the reply goes on once nothing in the message waits for the reader. |
| `edit` | `(messageId: string, text: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Rewrites a user message and asks again from there; later messages are dropped. |
| `getSnapshot` | `() => AgentChatSnapshot` | 是 |  |
| `reset` | `(next?: { id?: string \| null; messages?: AgentChatMessage[]; }) => void` | 是 | Switches to another conversation (or a new one), stopping any reply under way. |
| `retry` | `() => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Asks again for the answer to the last user message. |
| `send` | `(input: AgentSendInput) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Resolves false without sending while a reply is under way or the message is empty. |
| `skip` | `(questionId: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 |  |
| `stop` | `() => void` | 是 |  |
| `subscribe` | `(listener: () => void) => () => void` | 是 |  |

### formatCurrencyValue

Formats an amount in the configured currency, first dividing `minor` units by the currency's precision.

```typescript
formatCurrencyValue(value: number, config: I18nFormatConfig, locale: string, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions): string
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | `number` | 是 |  |
| `config` | `I18nFormatConfig` | 是 | 创建 Lumi 上下文时使用的 Provider 与运行环境配置。 |
| `locale` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `options` | `Intl.NumberFormatOptions \| undefined` | 否 |  |

`config` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `currency` | `string` | 是 | ISO 4217 code of the amounts, e.g. `CNY`. |
| `currencyUnit` | `"major" \| "minor"` | 是 | `major` when amounts are in the main unit (yuan), `minor` when in the smallest (fen). |
| `dateTimeInput` | `"iso-utc"` | 是 | The form datetimes arrive in: ISO 8601 UTC strings. |
| `timeZone` | `string` | 是 | IANA zone times are shown in, e.g. `Asia/Shanghai`. |

返回 `string`。

### handleAuthProviderError

Asks the auth provider what a failure means and signs the reader out when it says so; one error is handled once.

```typescript
handleAuthProviderError(authProvider: AuthErrorProvider | undefined, routerProvider: Pick<RouterProvider, "go"> | undefined, error: unknown): Promise<AuthErrorResponse | undefined>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `authProvider` | `AuthErrorProvider \| undefined` | 是 | 登录、退出、会话检查和读取身份。 |
| `routerProvider` | `Pick<RouterProvider, "go"> \| undefined` | 是 | 与框架无关的导航。 |
| `error` | `unknown` | 是 |  |

`authProvider` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `logout` | `(params?: any) => Promise<AuthActionResponse>` | 是 | Signs out and forgets the session. |
| `onError` | `((error: any) => Promise<AuthErrorResponse>) \| undefined` | 否 | What a failed data request means for the session, once `refresh` could not save it; `{ logout: true }` signs the reader out. |

`routerProvider` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `go` | `(config: GoConfig) => void` | 是 | Opens an address. |

返回 `Promise<AuthErrorResponse | undefined>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `error` | `Error \| undefined` | 否 | The failure, for the caller. |
| `logout` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | `true` signs the reader out. |
| `redirectTo` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the reader goes next. |

### importRecords

Creates the records one by one, or in `createMany` batches when
`batchSize` is set, going on past refused ones, and refreshes the resource
once at the end. A refused batch counts all its rows as failed.

```typescript
importRecords(context: LumiContext, client: QueryClient | undefined, records: readonly Record<string, unknown>[], options: ImportRecordsOptions): Promise<ImportResult>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `client` | `QueryClient \| undefined` | 是 |  |
| `records` | `readonly Record<string, unknown>[]` | 是 |  |
| `options` | `ImportRecordsOptions` | 是 |  |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `batchSize` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Records sent per `createMany` call, for backends whose bulk create takes or refuses a batch as a whole. Without it each record goes through `create`, so every refused row is reported on its own. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `onProgress` | `((progress: ImportProgress) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | 用于取消请求的 AbortSignal。 |

返回 `Promise<ImportResult>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `errors` | `{ row: number; message: string; }[]` | 是 | Rows the server refused, with its reason. `row` counts the file's records from 1: the header is not a record and blank lines are skipped, so it is the spreadsheet's line number minus the header only in a file without gaps. |
| `failed` | `number` | 是 |  |
| `processed` | `number` | 是 |  |
| `succeeded` | `number` | 是 |  |
| `total` | `number` | 是 |  |
| `uploaded` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | A backend import (`url`): percent of the file sent so far; the counts arrive with the answer. |

返回 `LumiQueryKey`。

### liveOptions

A hook's live options, without undefined entries.

```typescript
liveOptions(options: LiveOptions): LiveOptions
```

返回 `LiveOptions`。

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

### LumiProvider

The native json-render provider plus Lumi: registers the data actions and the `$t` directive, supplies the query client and reports failed actions.

```typescript
LumiProvider({ context, queryClient, ...native }: LumiProviderProps): ReactElement
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `__0` | `LumiProviderProps` | 是 |  |

`__0` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | 是 |  |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `directives` | `DirectiveDefinition<ZodType<unknown, unknown, $ZodTypeInternals<unknown, unknown>>>[] \| undefined` | 否 | Custom directives for user-defined `$`-prefixed dynamic values |
| `functions` | `Record<string, ComputedFunction> \| undefined` | 否 | Named functions for `$computed` expressions in props |
| `handlers` | `Record<string, (params: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<unknown> \| unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Action handlers |
| `initialState` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Initial state model (uncontrolled mode) |
| `navigate` | `((path: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation function |
| `onStateChange` | `((changes: Array<{ path: string; value: unknown; }>) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Callback when state changes (uncontrolled mode) |
| `queryClient` | `QueryClient \| undefined` | 否 | 复用已有的 TanStack Query Client。 |
| `registry` | `ComponentRegistry` | 是 | Component registry |
| `store` | `StateStore \| undefined` | 否 | External store (controlled mode). When provided, `initialState` and `onStateChange` are ignored. |
| `validationFunctions` | `Record<string, (value: unknown, args?: Record<string, unknown>) => boolean> \| undefined` | 否 | Custom validation functions |

返回 `ReactElement<unknown, string | JSXElementConstructor<any>>`。


| `cacheKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

| `functions` | `Record<string, ComputedFunction> \| undefined` | 否 | Named functions for `$computed` expressions in props |

| `initialState` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Top-level state entries set over the Spec's own `state` when the page mounts, e.g. `{ drawer: { id: 12 } }` for a page opened in a drawer. The document stays the same for every caller and is cached once. |

| `navigate` | `((path: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation function |

| `src` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
