### mergeUploadOptions

Merges upload options: the later ones override the earlier, except headers, where the earlier win, and `trustedOrigins`, which add up.

```typescript
mergeUploadOptions(injected: UploadOptions, defaults: UploadOptions, overrides: UploadOptions): UploadOptions
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `injected` | `UploadOptions` | 是 |  |
| `defaults` | `UploadOptions` | 是 |  |
| `overrides` | `UploadOptions` | 是 |  |

`injected` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `data` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Other form fields sent beside the file. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The data provider whose API root and request client are used. |
| `fieldName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Form field of the file; `file` by default. |
| `headers` | `UploadHeaders \| undefined` | 否 | Extra headers, or a function answering them; the reader's credentials are added by the request client. |
| `method` | `"POST" \| "PUT" \| "PATCH" \| undefined` | 否 | `POST` by default. |
| `sign` | `UploadSignOptions \| undefined` | 否 | Uploads straight to object storage with a ticket from the backend, instead of through `url`. |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | Aborts the upload. |
| `trustedOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Other sites an upload `url` may point at and be signed for; the API root and this site always are. |
| `url` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the file is sent, relative to the API root; `/upload` by default. |
| `withCredentials` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Sends cookies; follows the auth provider's credentials when left out. |

`defaults` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `data` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Other form fields sent beside the file. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The data provider whose API root and request client are used. |
| `fieldName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Form field of the file; `file` by default. |
| `headers` | `UploadHeaders \| undefined` | 否 | Extra headers, or a function answering them; the reader's credentials are added by the request client. |
| `method` | `"POST" \| "PUT" \| "PATCH" \| undefined` | 否 | `POST` by default. |
| `sign` | `UploadSignOptions \| undefined` | 否 | Uploads straight to object storage with a ticket from the backend, instead of through `url`. |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | Aborts the upload. |
| `trustedOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Other sites an upload `url` may point at and be signed for; the API root and this site always are. |
| `url` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the file is sent, relative to the API root; `/upload` by default. |
| `withCredentials` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Sends cookies; follows the auth provider's credentials when left out. |

`overrides` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `data` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Other form fields sent beside the file. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The data provider whose API root and request client are used. |
| `fieldName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Form field of the file; `file` by default. |
| `headers` | `UploadHeaders \| undefined` | 否 | Extra headers, or a function answering them; the reader's credentials are added by the request client. |
| `method` | `"POST" \| "PUT" \| "PATCH" \| undefined` | 否 | `POST` by default. |
| `sign` | `UploadSignOptions \| undefined` | 否 | Uploads straight to object storage with a ticket from the backend, instead of through `url`. |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | Aborts the upload. |
| `trustedOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Other sites an upload `url` may point at and be signed for; the API root and this site always are. |
| `url` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the file is sent, relative to the API root; `/upload` by default. |
| `withCredentials` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Sends cookies; follows the auth provider's credentials when left out. |

返回 `UploadOptions`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `data` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Other form fields sent beside the file. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The data provider whose API root and request client are used. |
| `fieldName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Form field of the file; `file` by default. |
| `headers` | `UploadHeaders \| undefined` | 否 | Extra headers, or a function answering them; the reader's credentials are added by the request client. |
| `method` | `"POST" \| "PUT" \| "PATCH" \| undefined` | 否 | `POST` by default. |
| `sign` | `UploadSignOptions \| undefined` | 否 | Uploads straight to object storage with a ticket from the backend, instead of through `url`. |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | Aborts the upload. |
| `trustedOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Other sites an upload `url` may point at and be signed for; the API root and this site always are. |
| `url` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the file is sent, relative to the API root; `/upload` by default. |
| `withCredentials` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Sends cookies; follows the auth provider's credentials when left out. |

### normalizeCustomParams

A custom request's params without their undefined entries, so equal requests share one cache key.

```typescript
normalizeCustomParams(params: CustomQueryParams): CustomQueryParams
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `params` | `CustomQueryParams` | 是 |  |

`params` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `filters` | `Filter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 筛选条件数组。 |
| `headers` | `Record<string, string> \| undefined` | 否 | 请求头。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `method` | `"GET" \| "POST" \| "PUT" \| "PATCH" \| "DELETE" \| undefined` | 否 | `GET` by default. |
| `payload` | `unknown` | 否 | The request body; not allowed with `GET`. |
| `query` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Query parameters. |
| `sorters` | `Sorter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 排序条件数组。 |
| `url` | `string` | 是 | The endpoint, relative to the API root. |

返回 `CustomQueryParams`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `filters` | `Filter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 筛选条件数组。 |
| `headers` | `Record<string, string> \| undefined` | 否 | 请求头。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `method` | `"GET" \| "POST" \| "PUT" \| "PATCH" \| "DELETE" \| undefined` | 否 | `GET` by default. |
| `payload` | `unknown` | 否 | The request body; not allowed with `GET`. |
| `query` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Query parameters. |
| `sorters` | `Sorter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 排序条件数组。 |
| `url` | `string` | 是 | The endpoint, relative to the API root. |

### parseAgentStream

Reads Server-Sent Events from a byte stream and yields each `data:` payload
parsed as a chunk. Comments, `event:` and `id:` lines are ignored; a payload
spread over several `data:` lines is joined with line breaks, as in the SSE
specification. Stops at `[DONE]` or the end of the stream.

```typescript
parseAgentStream(body: ReadableStream<Uint8Array>, signal?: AbortSignal): AsyncGenerator<AgentChunk>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `body` | `ReadableStream<Uint8Array<ArrayBufferLike>>` | 是 |  |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | 用于取消请求的 AbortSignal。 |

返回 `AsyncGenerator<AgentChunk, any, any>`。

### parseCsv

RFC 4180 CSV: quoted fields, doubled quotes, CRLF or LF; a leading BOM is dropped.

```typescript
parseCsv(text: string): string[][]
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `text` | `string` | 是 |  |

返回 `string[][]`。

### parseLumiError

Lumi's error convention: `{ message, code, data, errors: [{ path, message }] }`.
A backend with another shape supplies its own `parseError`.

```typescript
parseLumiError(body: unknown): HttpErrorDetails
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `body` | `unknown` | 是 |  |

返回 `HttpErrorDetails`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `code` | `unknown` | 否 |  |
| `data` | `unknown` | 否 |  |
| `errors` | `HttpFieldError[] \| undefined` | 否 | Field errors; the form puts each under its field. |
| `message` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

```typescript
pathMatchesFields(path: string, fields: readonly string[]): boolean
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `path` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `fields` | `readonly string[]` | 是 |  |

返回 `boolean`。

The action a Lumi query key was made for (`list`, `one`, ...), as createQueryKey places it.


返回 `unknown`。

### queryResets

How many times this client's Lumi queries were reset for a new tenant; lists drop their kept rows across one.

```typescript
queryResets(client: QueryClient): number
```

返回 `number`。

### readMany

Reads several records: through `getMany` when the provider has it, else one
`getOne` per id. Hooks and the JSON `Query` share it.

```typescript
readMany<T = any>(provider: DataProvider, params: GetManyParams): Promise<ManyResult<T>>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `provider` | `DataProvider` | 是 |  |
| `params` | `GetManyParams` | 是 |  |

`provider` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `create` | `<T = any, V = any>(params: CreateParams<V>) => Promise<SingleResult<T>>` | 是 | Creates a record from `variables` and answers the saved one. |
| `createMany` | `(<T = any, V = any>(params: CreateManyParams<V>) => Promise<ManyResult<T>>) \| undefined` | 否 | Creates several records in one request; without it they are created one by one. |
| `custom` | `(<T = any>(params: CustomParams) => Promise<SingleResult<T>>) \| undefined` | 否 | A request outside the resource conventions; the `request` action, `useCustom` and `useCustomMutation` need it. |
| `deleteMany` | `(<T = any, V = any>(params: DeleteManyParams<V>) => Promise<ManyResult<T>>) \| undefined` | 否 | Deletes several records; without it they are deleted one by one. |
| `deleteOne` | `<T = any, V = any>(params: DeleteParams<V>) => Promise<SingleResult<T>>` | 是 | Deletes the record `id`. |
| `getApiUrl` | `(() => string) \| undefined` | 否 | The API root, for callers that build full addresses such as uploads. |
| `getList` | `<T = any>(params: GetListParams) => Promise<GetListResult<T>>` | 是 | One page of a resource, filtered and sorted, or all of it with `pagination: false`; `total` counts every match. |
| `getMany` | `(<T = any>(params: GetManyParams) => Promise<ManyResult<T>>) \| undefined` | 否 | Several records by id in one request; without it each id is read with `getOne`. |
| `getOne` | `<T = any>(params: GetOneParams) => Promise<SingleResult<T>>` | 是 | One record by id. |
| `http` | `HttpClient \| undefined` | 否 | The client it sends through; uploads and the agent reuse its credentials. |
| `update` | `<T = any, V = any>(params: UpdateParams<V>) => Promise<SingleResult<T>>` | 是 | Updates the record `id` with `variables` and answers the saved one. |
| `updateMany` | `(<T = any, V = any>(params: UpdateManyParams<V>) => Promise<ManyResult<T>>) \| undefined` | 否 | Applies the same `variables` to several records; without it they are updated one by one. |

`params` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ids` | `ID[]` | 是 | 记录主键数组。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | 用于取消请求的 AbortSignal。 |

返回 `Promise<ManyResult<T>>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `data` | `T[]` | 是 | The records. |
| `raw` | `unknown` | 否 | The backend's whole answer. |

### readRecords

The records in a CSV or JSON file. A CSV header names each column by field
or by its declared label; without declared columns every header is a field.
Columns the file does not have are left out of the records.

```typescript
readRecords(file: Pick<TransferFile, "name" | "content">, columns?: readonly TransferColumn[]): Record<string, unknown>[]
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `file` | `Pick<TransferFile, "name" \| "content">` | 是 |  |
| `columns` | `readonly TransferColumn[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |

`file` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `content` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `name` | `string` | 是 |  |

返回 `Record<string, unknown>[]`。

### recordAudit

Hands an accepted write to the audit log provider. The write already
happened, so a log that fails is reported on the console, not to the reader.

```typescript
recordAudit(context: LumiContext, event: AuditLogEvent, client?: QueryClient, dataProviderName?: string): void
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `event` | `AuditLogEvent` | 是 |  |
| `client` | `QueryClient \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

`event` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `action` | `string` | 是 | `create`, `update`, `delete`, the `*Many` forms, `import`, or an app's own name. |
| `data` | `unknown` | 否 | The values sent; for an import, its counts. |
| `ids` | `ID[] \| undefined` | 否 | The record ids the write touched, when known. |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | The write's own `meta`. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | The resource written to. |

返回 `void`。

### registerAgentSession

Makes a mounted session reachable from JSON actions. `owner` is the page
state the session writes to (its store identity) and `path` the state path
it projects to, which actions name as `session`.

```typescript
registerAgentSession(owner: object, path: string, session: AgentChatSession): () => void
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `owner` | `object` | 是 |  |
| `path` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `session` | `AgentChatSession` | 是 |  |

`session` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `answer` | `(questionId: string, answer: string \| string[]) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 |  |
| `approve` | `(approvalId: string, approved: boolean, reason?: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Decides an approval; the reply goes on once nothing in the message waits for the reader. |
| `edit` | `(messageId: string, text: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Rewrites a user message and asks again from there; later messages are dropped. |
| `getSnapshot` | `() => AgentChatSnapshot` | 是 |  |
| `reset` | `(next?: { id?: string \| null; messages?: AgentChatMessage[]; }) => void` | 是 | Switches to another conversation (or a new one), stopping any reply under way. |
| `retry` | `() => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Asks again for the answer to the last user message. |
| `send` | `(input: AgentSendInput) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Resolves false without sending while a reply is under way or the message is empty. |
| `skip` | `(questionId: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 |  |
| `stop` | `() => void` | 是 |  |
| `subscribe` | `(listener: () => void) => () => void` | 是 |  |

返回 `() => void`。

The current errors with those of `fields` replaced by the new ones; every error is replaced when no fields are named.

### resetSessionQueries

Cancels and drops every Lumi query after a session change, then fetches the mounted ones again, or only empties them with `refetch: false`.

```typescript
resetSessionQueries(client: QueryClient, result: AuthActionResponse, refetch?: boolean): Promise<void>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `client` | `QueryClient` | 是 |  |
| `result` | `AuthActionResponse` | 是 |  |
| `refetch` | `boolean` | 否 |  |

`result` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `error` | `Error \| undefined` | 否 | Why it failed. |
| `redirectTo` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the reader goes next. |
| `success` | `boolean` | 是 | `false` keeps the current session; the sign-in page shows `error`. |

返回 `Promise<void>`。

A record as a select option, by its label, value, description and image fields.

### runExport

Hands the export file to `save` and returns it: the backend's when `url` is
set, else one written here from every matching record.

```typescript
runExport(context: LumiContext, client: QueryClient | undefined, input: RunExportOptions, save: TransferHost["save"]): Promise<TransferFile | TransferDownload>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `client` | `QueryClient \| undefined` | 是 |  |
| `input` | `RunExportOptions` | 是 |  |
| `save` | `(file: TransferFile \| TransferDownload) => void` | 是 |  |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `columns` | `readonly TransferColumn[] \| undefined` | 否 | The columns of the file, in order; every field of the records when left out. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `filename` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Name of the saved file without its extension; the resource name by default. |
| `filters` | `Filter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 筛选条件数组。 |
| `format` | `(string & {}) \| TransferFormat \| undefined` | 否 | `csv` or `json` for a file written here; with `url`, any name the backend knows (`xlsx`…). |
| `maxItems` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Stops after this many records, so an export cannot run away. |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `method` | `"GET" \| "POST" \| undefined` | 否 | `POST` (default) sends the values as JSON; `GET` as the query, `filters`, `sorters` and `columns` as JSON text. |
| `onProgress` | `((progress: { loaded: number; total?: number; }) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `pageSize` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Records asked for per request. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | 用于取消请求的 AbortSignal。 |
| `sorters` | `Sorter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 排序条件数组。 |
| `url` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The backend builds the file: this endpoint (relative to the API root) is sent `{ filters, sorters, columns, format, ...meta }` and answers the file as a stream, in any format. Without it the records are loaded page by page and the file is written in the browser. |

返回 `Promise<TransferFile | TransferDownload>`。

### runImport

Reads the file, creates its records and tells the reader how it went: a
success toast, or a warning notice naming the first refused rows.

```typescript
runImport(context: LumiContext, client: QueryClient | undefined, file: Pick<TransferFile, "name" | "content">, input: RunImportOptions): Promise<ImportResult>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `client` | `QueryClient \| undefined` | 是 |  |
| `file` | `Pick<TransferFile, "name" \| "content">` | 是 |  |
| `input` | `RunImportOptions` | 是 |  |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

`file` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `content` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `name` | `string` | 是 |  |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `batchSize` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Records sent per `createMany` call, for backends whose bulk create takes or refuses a batch as a whole. Without it each record goes through `create`, so every refused row is reported on its own. |
| `columns` | `readonly TransferColumn[] \| undefined` | 否 | Maps the file's headings to record fields; headings are taken as field names when left out. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `onProgress` | `((progress: ImportProgress) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | 用于取消请求的 AbortSignal。 |
| `url` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The backend performs the import: the chosen file is uploaded to this endpoint (relative to the API root) as the form field `file`, with `meta` beside it, and the answer `{ data: { total, succeeded, failed, errors } }` is the result. Without it the file is read here and each record created. |

返回 `Promise<ImportResult>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `errors` | `{ row: number; message: string; }[]` | 是 | Rows the server refused, with its reason. `row` counts the file's records from 1: the header is not a record and blank lines are skipped, so it is the spreadsheet's line number minus the header only in a file without gaps. |
| `failed` | `number` | 是 |  |
| `processed` | `number` | 是 |  |
| `succeeded` | `number` | 是 |  |
| `total` | `number` | 是 |  |
| `uploaded` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | A backend import (`url`): percent of the file sent so far; the counts arrive with the answer. |

### runMutationMode

Runs a write in its mutation mode. Undoable needs a notification provider
to offer Undo; without one it behaves as optimistic. The same function
serves the data hooks and the JSON `update` and `delete` actions.

```typescript
runMutationMode<T>(context: LumiContext, client: QueryClient, options: RunMutationOptions<T>): Promise<T>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `client` | `QueryClient` | 是 |  |
| `options` | `RunMutationOptions<T>` | 是 |  |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `change` | `OptimisticChange` | 是 |  |
| `mode` | `MutationMode \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `request` | `() => Promise<T>` | 是 | Sends the write; for undoable mode only once the wait is over. |
| `target` | `{ resource: string; dataProviderName?: string; }` | 是 |  |
| `undoableTimeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `Promise<T>`。

### sendFile

Sends one file as a form to the backend, with upload progress, and answers
the parsed response body. The data provider's client prepares the request
as it prepares any other (credentials, tenant, `onRequest`) and sees the
answer (`onResponse`). Uploads and backend imports share it.

```typescript
sendFile(context: LumiContext | undefined, file: File, options: UploadOptions, onProgress?: (loaded: number, total: number) => void): Promise<unknown>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `context` | `LumiContext \| undefined` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `file` | `File` | 是 |  |
| `options` | `UploadOptions` | 是 |  |
| `onProgress` | `(loaded: number, total: number) => void` | 否 |  |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `data` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Other form fields sent beside the file. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The data provider whose API root and request client are used. |
| `fieldName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Form field of the file; `file` by default. |
| `headers` | `UploadHeaders \| undefined` | 否 | Extra headers, or a function answering them; the reader's credentials are added by the request client. |
| `method` | `"POST" \| "PUT" \| "PATCH" \| undefined` | 否 | `POST` by default. |
| `sign` | `UploadSignOptions \| undefined` | 否 | Uploads straight to object storage with a ticket from the backend, instead of through `url`. |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | Aborts the upload. |
| `trustedOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Other sites an upload `url` may point at and be signed for; the API root and this site always are. |
| `url` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the file is sent, relative to the API root; `/upload` by default. |
| `withCredentials` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Sends cookies; follows the auth provider's credentials when left out. |

返回 `Promise<unknown>`。

### toastError

Reports a failure as a danger toast. Every automatic error report goes
through here: unhandled action failures, React mutations and uploads.
Cancellations and duplicate submits stay silent.


| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `context` | `Pick<LumiContext, "i18nProvider" \| "toastProvider">` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `error` | `unknown` | 是 |  |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |

返回 `void`。

### uploadImport

Uploads the file for the backend to import, reporting how much of it has
been sent, then reads the outcome from the answer and refreshes the
resource. A backend that answers no counts is taken to have accepted the file.

```typescript
uploadImport(context: LumiContext, client: QueryClient | undefined, file: File, input: RunImportOptions & { url: string; }): Promise<ImportResult>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `context` | `LumiContext` | 是 | `createLumi` 创建的共享上下文。 |
| `client` | `QueryClient \| undefined` | 是 |  |
| `file` | `File` | 是 |  |
| `input` | `RunImportOptions & { url: string; }` | 是 |  |

`context` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `batchSize` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Records sent per `createMany` call, for backends whose bulk create takes or refuses a batch as a whole. Without it each record goes through `create`, so every refused row is reported on its own. |
| `columns` | `readonly TransferColumn[] \| undefined` | 否 | Maps the file's headings to record fields; headings are taken as field names when left out. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `onProgress` | `((progress: ImportProgress) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | 用于取消请求的 AbortSignal。 |
| `url` | `string` | 是 | The backend performs the import: the chosen file is uploaded to this endpoint (relative to the API root) as the form field `file`, with `meta` beside it, and the answer `{ data: { total, succeeded, failed, errors } }` is the result. Without it the file is read here and each record created. |

返回 `Promise<ImportResult>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `errors` | `{ row: number; message: string; }[]` | 是 | Rows the server refused, with its reason. `row` counts the file's records from 1: the header is not a record and blank lines are skipped, so it is the spreadsheet's line number minus the header only in a file without gaps. |
| `failed` | `number` | 是 |  |
| `processed` | `number` | 是 |  |
| `succeeded` | `number` | 是 |  |
| `total` | `number` | 是 |  |
| `uploaded` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | A backend import (`url`): percent of the file sent so far; the counts arrive with the answer. |

### useAgentApprovalMode

The approval mode every agent conversation shares: `ask` waits for the
reader before changes, `auto` acts at once. It is sent with each request so
the server applies the same rule to its own approvals, and kept between visits.

```typescript
useAgentApprovalMode(): [AgentApprovalMode, (mode: AgentApprovalMode) => void]
```

返回 `[AgentApprovalMode, (mode: AgentApprovalMode) => void]`。

### useAgentChat

A conversation with an agent endpoint. The reply streams in as an AI SDK UI
message stream; requests are signed by the auth provider through the data
provider's HttpClient, carry the configured agent headers and go through
the auth provider's error handling like any other Lumi request. One session
serves the hook and the JSON AgentSession component.

```typescript
useAgentChat(options?: UseAgentChatOptions): AgentChat
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `UseAgentChatOptions` | 否 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `body` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Extra fields sent with every request, e.g. the model. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The data provider whose API root and request client the chat uses. |
| `endpoint` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to the configured `agent.endpoint`, then `/agent/chat`. |
| `headers` | `AgentHeaders \| undefined` | 否 | Extra request headers; the reader's credentials are added by the request client. |
| `onData` | `((chunk: AgentChunk) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called for each `data-*` chunk of the stream. |
| `onError` | `((error: Error) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called when a reply fails. |
| `onFinish` | `((event: AgentFinishEvent) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called when a reply ends, however it ended. |
| `prepareBody` | `((request: AgentRequest, body: Record<string, unknown>) => Record<string, unknown>) \| undefined` | 否 | Last change to the request body before it is sent. |
| `resource` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A DataProvider resource whose `getOne` returns a conversation's messages; refreshed after each reply. |
| `sessionId` | `string \| null \| undefined` | 否 | The conversation to open; a change switches to it, empty starts a new one. |
| `transport` | `AgentTransport \| undefined` | 否 | Replaces the HTTP transport, e.g. for a WebSocket or an in-page agent. |
| `ui` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Lets the agent see and act on the front end through the Lumi agent bridge: each request carries the UI snapshot (`ui`), the client tools (`clientTools`) and the approval mode (`approvalMode`), and calls to those tools run here. Defaults to true. |

返回 `AgentChat`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `answer` | `(questionId: string, answer: string \| string[]) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Answers a question the agent asked. |
| `approve` | `(approvalId: string, approved: boolean, reason?: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Answers an approval request, accepting or refusing it. |
| `edit` | `(messageId: string, text: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Rewrites a user message and answers from there. |
| `error` | `string \| null` | 是 | Why the last reply failed. |
| `id` | `string` | 是 | The conversation's id. |
| `loading` | `boolean` | 是 | True while the conversation's earlier messages load. |
| `messages` | `AgentChatMessage[]` | 是 | The conversation so far. |
| `reset` | `(next?: { id?: string \| null; messages?: AgentChatMessage[]; }) => void` | 是 | Starts a new, empty conversation. |
| `retry` | `() => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Asks again for an answer to the last user message. |
| `send` | `(input: AgentSendInput) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Sends a message; ignored while a reply is under way or when it is empty. |
| `session` | `AgentChatSession` | 是 | The session behind the chat, for calls the hook does not surface. |
| `skip` | `(questionId: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Skips a question the agent asked. |
| `status` | `AgentChatStatus` | 是 | `ready`, `submitted` while a reply is requested, `streaming` while it arrives, or `error`. |
| `stop` | `() => void` | 是 | Stops the reply under way, keeping what arrived. |
| `streaming` | `boolean` | 是 | True while a reply is requested or streaming. |

### useAuditLogList

The audit log of a resource or record, newest first, from the audit log
provider. It refreshes after every write to the resource. Without a
provider that lists, it stays empty and idle.

```typescript
useAuditLogList(input: UseAuditLogListOptions): UseQueryResult<{ data: AuditLogEntry[]; total?: number; }, Error>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `input` | `UseAuditLogListOptions` | 是 |  |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `enabled` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | 是否允许自动执行查询。 |
| `id` | `ID \| undefined` | 否 | One record's history; the whole resource's without it. |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `pagination` | `Pagination \| undefined` | 否 | 分页参数；传 false 关闭分页。 |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |

返回 `UseQueryResult<{ data: AuditLogEntry[]; total?: number; }, Error>`。

### useCan

Asks the access provider whether the reader may do `action` on `resource`; `data.can` is true without an access provider. The answer is cached until the session changes.

```typescript
useCan(params: CanParams, enabled?: boolean): UseQueryResult<CanResponse, Error>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `params` | `CanParams` | 是 |  |
| `enabled` | `boolean` | 否 | 是否允许自动执行查询。 |

`params` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `action` | `string` | 是 | The action, e.g. `list`, `show`, `create`, `edit`, `delete`, or a name of the app's own. |
| `params` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Extra conditions for a check on one record, e.g. `{ id: 12 }`. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | The resource asked about, e.g. `orders` or `system/users`. |

返回 `UseQueryResult<CanResponse, Error>`。

### useChangeLocale

Returns `changeLocale(locale)`, which switches the i18n provider's locale.

```typescript
useChangeLocale(): (locale: string, params?: unknown) => Promise<void>
```

返回 `(locale: string, params?: unknown) => Promise<void>`。

### useCheck

Whether a session exists, from `authProvider.check`; always authenticated without an auth provider.

```typescript
useCheck(): UseQueryResult<CheckResponse, Error>
```

返回 `UseQueryResult<CheckResponse, Error>`。

### useClient

The TanStack `QueryClient` Lumi caches in, for direct cache work.

```typescript
useClient(): QueryClient
```

返回 `QueryClient`。

### useCreate

Creates a record through `create`, then refetches the resource's queries. Returns a mutation: `mutate(values)`.

```typescript
useCreate<T = any, V = any>(options: MutationOptions<SingleResult<T>, V>): UseMutationResult<SingleResult<T>, Error, V>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `MutationOptions<SingleResult<T>, V>` | 是 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `mutationMode` | `MutationMode \| undefined` | 否 | Updates and deletes only: `optimistic` shows the change at once and rolls it back on failure; `undoable` also waits `undoableTimeout` with an Undo notice before sending (Undo rejects with MutationUndoneError, unreported). Defaults to the context's `mutationMode`, else `pessimistic`. |
| `onError` | `((error: Error, variables: V) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Replaces the default report: without it a failure is shown as a danger toast. |
| `onSuccess` | `((data: SingleResult<T>, variables: V) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Called with the saved data once the write succeeded. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | The resource written to; its lists, records and aggregates refetch after a success. |
| `undoableTimeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Milliseconds an `undoable` write waits for Undo before it is sent; 5000 by default. |

返回 `UseMutationResult<SingleResult<T>, Error, V>`。

### useCreateMany

Creates several records: one `createMany` call when the provider has it, else one `create` per record.

```typescript
useCreateMany<T = any, V = any>(options: MutationOptions<ManyResult<T>, V[]>): UseMutationResult<ManyResult<T>, Error, V[]>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `MutationOptions<ManyResult<T>, V[]>` | 是 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `mutationMode` | `MutationMode \| undefined` | 否 | Updates and deletes only: `optimistic` shows the change at once and rolls it back on failure; `undoable` also waits `undoableTimeout` with an Undo notice before sending (Undo rejects with MutationUndoneError, unreported). Defaults to the context's `mutationMode`, else `pessimistic`. |
| `onError` | `((error: Error, variables: V[]) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Replaces the default report: without it a failure is shown as a danger toast. |
| `onSuccess` | `((data: ManyResult<T>, variables: V[]) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Called with the saved data once the write succeeded. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | The resource written to; its lists, records and aggregates refetch after a success. |
| `undoableTimeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Milliseconds an `undoable` write waits for Undo before it is sent; 5000 by default. |

返回 `UseMutationResult<ManyResult<T>, Error, V[]>`。

### useCustom

Reads from an endpoint outside the resource conventions through the provider's `custom`; cached by every parameter.

```typescript
useCustom<T = any>(input: UseCustomOptions): UseQueryResult<SingleResult<T>, Error>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `input` | `UseCustomOptions` | 是 |  |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `enabled` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | 是否允许自动执行查询。 |
| `filters` | `Filter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 筛选条件数组。 |
| `headers` | `Record<string, string> \| undefined` | 否 | 请求头。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `method` | `"GET" \| "POST" \| "PUT" \| "PATCH" \| "DELETE" \| undefined` | 否 | `GET` by default. |
| `payload` | `unknown` | 否 | The request body; not allowed with `GET`. |
| `query` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Query parameters. |
| `sorters` | `Sorter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 排序条件数组。 |
| `url` | `string` | 是 | The endpoint, relative to the API root. |

返回 `UseQueryResult<SingleResult<T>, Error>`。

### useCustomMutation

Writes through the provider's `custom`: `build` turns the mutation's variables into the request, and `resource`, when given, is refetched after it succeeds.

```typescript
useCustomMutation<T = any, V = any>(options: Omit<MutationOptions<SingleResult<T>, V>, "resource"> & { resource?: string; build: (variables: V) => Omit<CustomParams, "signal">; }): UseMutationResult<SingleResult<T>, Error, V>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `Omit<MutationOptions<SingleResult<T>, V>, "resource"> & { resource?: string; build: (variables: V) => Omit<CustomParams, "signal">; }` | 是 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `build` | `(variables: V) => Omit<CustomParams, "signal">` | 是 |  |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `mutationMode` | `MutationMode \| undefined` | 否 | Updates and deletes only: `optimistic` shows the change at once and rolls it back on failure; `undoable` also waits `undoableTimeout` with an Undo notice before sending (Undo rejects with MutationUndoneError, unreported). Defaults to the context's `mutationMode`, else `pessimistic`. |
| `onError` | `((error: Error, variables: V) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Replaces the default report: without it a failure is shown as a danger toast. |
| `onSuccess` | `((data: SingleResult<T>, variables: V) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Called with the saved data once the write succeeded. |
| `resource` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `undoableTimeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Milliseconds an `undoable` write waits for Undo before it is sent; 5000 by default. |

返回 `UseMutationResult<SingleResult<T>, Error, V>`。

### useDataProviderName

`explicit`, else the enclosing scope's provider, else undefined (the default).

```typescript
useDataProviderName(explicit?: string): string | undefined
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `explicit` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `string | undefined`。

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `MutationOptions<SingleResult<T>, DeleteVariables<V>>` | 是 |  |

| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `mutationMode` | `MutationMode \| undefined` | 否 | Updates and deletes only: `optimistic` shows the change at once and rolls it back on failure; `undoable` also waits `undoableTimeout` with an Undo notice before sending (Undo rejects with MutationUndoneError, unreported). Defaults to the context's `mutationMode`, else `pessimistic`. |
| `onError` | `((error: Error, variables: DeleteVariables<V>) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Replaces the default report: without it a failure is shown as a danger toast. |
| `onSuccess` | `((data: SingleResult<T>, variables: DeleteVariables<V>) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Called with the saved data once the write succeeded. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | The resource written to; its lists, records and aggregates refetch after a success. |
| `undoableTimeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Milliseconds an `undoable` write waits for Undo before it is sent; 5000 by default. |

返回 `UseMutationResult<SingleResult<T>, Error, DeleteVariables<V>>`。

### useDialog

Confirm, alert and prompt dialogs drawn by the host, following its theme: `confirm` resolves true when the reader accepts.

```typescript
useDialog(): { confirm: (options: DialogOptions) => Promise<boolean>; alert: (options: DialogOptions) => Promise<void>; prompt: (options: DialogOptions) => Promise<unknown>; }
```

返回 `{ confirm: (options: DialogOptions) => Promise<boolean>; alert: (options: DialogOptions) => Promise<void>; prompt: (options: DialogOptions) => Promise<unknown>; }`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `alert` | `(options: DialogOptions) => Promise<void>` | 是 |  |
| `confirm` | `(options: DialogOptions) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 |  |
| `prompt` | `(options: DialogOptions) => Promise<unknown>` | 是 |  |

### useDrawer

Opens a drawer with content rendered by `render`, from the side `placement` names.

```typescript
useDrawer(): { open: (options: OverlayOpenOptions & { placement?: "left" | "right" | "top" | "bottom"; }) => OverlayHandle; }
```

返回 `{ open: (options: OverlayOpenOptions & { placement?: "left" | "right" | "top" | "bottom"; }) => OverlayHandle; }`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `open` | `(options: OverlayOpenOptions & { placement?: "left" \| "right" \| "top" \| "bottom"; }) => OverlayHandle` | 是 |  |

### useExport

Exports a resource's matching records as CSV or JSON, or saves the file the backend builds at `url`, the same way as the `exportList` action.

```typescript
useExport(input: UseExportOptions): UseExportReturn
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `input` | `UseExportOptions` | 是 |  |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `columns` | `readonly TransferColumn[] \| undefined` | 否 | The columns of the file, in order; every field of the records when left out. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `filename` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Name of the saved file without its extension; the resource name by default. |
| `filters` | `Filter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 筛选条件数组。 |
| `format` | `(string & {}) \| TransferFormat \| undefined` | 否 | `csv` or `json` for a file written here; with `url`, any name the backend knows (`xlsx`…). |
| `maxItems` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Stops after this many records, so an export cannot run away. |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `method` | `"GET" \| "POST" \| undefined` | 否 | `POST` (default) sends the values as JSON; `GET` as the query, `filters`, `sorters` and `columns` as JSON text. |
| `pageSize` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Records asked for per request. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `save` | `((file: TransferFile \| TransferDownload) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Where the file goes; the browser download by default. |
| `sorters` | `Sorter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 排序条件数组。 |
| `url` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The backend builds the file: this endpoint (relative to the API root) is sent `{ filters, sorters, columns, format, ...meta }` and answers the file as a stream, in any format. Without it the records are loaded page by page and the file is written in the browser. |

返回 `UseExportReturn`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `error` | `Error \| undefined` | 否 | Why the last export failed. |
| `isLoading` | `boolean` | 是 | True while the export runs. |
| `progress` | `{ loaded: number; total?: number; } \| undefined` | 否 | Records loaded so far and their total; with `url`, bytes received and the file's size when the backend sends `Content-Length`. |
| `trigger` | `() => Promise<TransferFile \| TransferDownload \| undefined>` | 是 | Loads the records and saves the file; the newest call wins over an earlier one still loading. |

### useForgotPassword

Starts a password reset through `authProvider.forgotPassword`.

```typescript
useForgotPassword(): UseMutationResult<AuthActionResponse, Error, any>
```

返回 `UseMutationResult<AuthActionResponse, Error, any>`。

### useForm

A form over one record: loads it when `id` is given, keeps the edited values in page state, validates, and saves once through the same path as the `submitForm` action.

```typescript
useForm<T extends Record<string, any> = Record<string, any>>(input: UseFormOptions<T>): UseFormReturn<T>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `input` | `UseFormOptions<T>` | 是 |  |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `draft` | `FormDraftOptions<T> \| undefined` | 否 | Keeps unsaved values in the browser so they can be restored later. |
| `groups` | `Record<string, FormGroupDefinition> \| undefined` | 否 | Named groups of fields, such as the steps of a wizard; `groupErrors` reports each group's errors. |
| `id` | `ID \| undefined` | 否 | With an id the record is loaded and the form updates it; without one it creates. |
| `initialValues` | `Partial<T> \| undefined` | 否 | Values of a new record. An edited record initializes once it has loaded, and later refetches leave the edits alone. |
| `meta` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | The resource the form saves to. |
| `rules` | `unknown` | 否 | Business rules for the context's Validator, e.g. a JSON Schema. Leave it out when no validator is configured. |
| `valuesPath` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the values live in page state; generated when left out. JSON elements bound to the same path edit the same form. |

返回 `UseFormReturn<T>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `draft` | `FormDraftControl \| undefined` | 否 | Draft controls, present when the `draft` option is set. |
| `errors` | `FormValidationIssue[]` | 是 | Every current error, the server's field errors included. |
| `firstError` | `(fields?: readonly string[]) => string \| undefined` | 是 | The path of the first field in error, among `fields` when given. |
| `groupErrors` | `Record<string, FormErrorGroup>` | 是 | The errors of each group in `groups`, with their count and first field. |
| `isDirty` | `boolean` | 是 | Whether the values differ from those loaded or last saved. |
| `isSubmitting` | `boolean` | 是 | True while a save is under way; another `submit` then sends nothing. |
| `reset` | `(next?: Partial<T> \| undefined) => void` | 是 | Returns to the initial values, or to `next`. |
| `setValue` | `(name: string \| keyof T, value: unknown) => void` | 是 | Sets one field; `name` may be a dotted path such as `items.0.sku`. |
| `setValues` | `(next: T \| ((current: T) => T)) => void` | 是 | Replaces every value, or derives them from the current ones. |
| `submit` | `() => Promise<SingleResult<T>>` | 是 | Validates, then saves; rejects when validation fails or the server refuses, with the errors already in `errors`. |
| `validate` | `(options?: FormValidateOptions) => Promise<FormValidationIssue[]>` | 是 | Validates all fields, or only `fields`, without saving; a wizard calls it on "Next". |
| `values` | `T` | 是 | The current values. |
| `valuesPath` | `string` | 是 | The page state path the values live at. |


```typescript
useFormatNumber(): (value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions) => string
```

返回 `(value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions) => string`。

### useFormatRelativeTime

Formats a relative time such as "3 days ago" for the current locale.

```typescript
useFormatRelativeTime(): (value: number, unit: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatUnit, options?: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatOptions) => string
```

Another name for `useTranslate`.

```typescript
useI18n(): (key: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, defaultMessage?: string) => string
```
