### useIdentity

The current user, from `authProvider.getIdentity`; `null` when signed out or when the provider has none.

```typescript
useIdentity<T = any>(): UseQueryResult<T | null, Error>
```

返回 `UseQueryResult<T | null, Error>`。

### useImport

Creates the records of a CSV or JSON file with progress, or uploads the file for the backend to import at `url`, the same way as the `importList` action.

```typescript
useImport(input: UseImportOptions): UseImportReturn
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `input` | `UseImportOptions` | 是 |  |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accept` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | File types the picker offers, e.g. `.csv,.json`. |
| `batchSize` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Records sent per `createMany` call, for backends whose bulk create takes or refuses a batch as a whole. Without it each record goes through `create`, so every refused row is reported on its own. |
| `columns` | `readonly TransferColumn[] \| undefined` | 否 | Maps the file's headings to record fields; headings are taken as field names when left out. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `onFinish` | `((result: ImportResult) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called with the result once the import ended. |
| `pick` | `((accept?: string) => Promise<Pick<TransferFile, "name" \| "content"> \| undefined>) \| undefined` | 否 | Chooses and reads the file of a browser import; the browser's picker by default. |
| `pickFile` | `((accept?: string) => Promise<File \| undefined>) \| undefined` | 否 | Chooses the file of a backend import (`url`); the browser's picker by default. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `url` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The backend performs the import: the chosen file is uploaded to this endpoint (relative to the API root) as the form field `file`, with `meta` beside it, and the answer `{ data: { total, succeeded, failed, errors } }` is the result. Without it the file is read here and each record created. |

返回 `UseImportReturn`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `cancel` | `() => void` | 是 | Stops after the batch under way. |
| `error` | `Error \| undefined` | 否 | Why the last import failed as a whole. |
| `isLoading` | `boolean` | 是 | True while the import runs. |
| `progress` | `ImportProgress \| undefined` | 否 | Records processed, accepted and refused so far; with `url`, the percent of the file uploaded. |
| `result` | `ImportResult \| undefined` | 否 | How the last import went: totals and the refused rows. |
| `trigger` | `(file?: Pick<TransferFile, "name" \| "content"> \| File) => Promise<ImportResult \| undefined>` | 是 | Opens the file picker, or imports the given file; resolves undefined when the reader cancels. |

### useInfiniteList

Reads a resource page after page for "load more" lists: `fetchNextPage()` loads the next, `data.pages` holds those loaded.

```typescript
useInfiniteList<T = any>(input: UseInfiniteListOptions): UseInfiniteQueryResult<InfiniteData<GetListResult<T>>, Error>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `input` | `UseInfiniteListOptions` | 是 |  |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `enabled` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | 是否允许自动执行查询。 |
| `filters` | `Filter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 筛选条件数组。 |
| `liveMode` | `LiveMode \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to the context's `liveMode`, which is `auto` with a liveProvider. |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `onLiveEvent` | `((event: LiveEvent) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called for each event of the resource, in `auto` and `manual` mode. |
| `pagination` | `{ pageSize?: number; } \| undefined` | 否 | 分页参数；传 false 关闭分页。 |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `sorters` | `Sorter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 排序条件数组。 |

返回 `UseInfiniteQueryResult<InfiniteData<GetListResult<T>, unknown>, Error>`。

### useInvalidate

Returns `invalidate({ resource?, dataProviderName? })`: refetches a resource's queries, a whole data provider's without `resource`, or every Lumi query without either; like the `invalidate` action.

```typescript
useInvalidate(): (params: { resource?: string; dataProviderName?: string; }) => Promise<void>
```

返回 `(params: { resource?: string; dataProviderName?: string; }) => Promise<void>`。

### useList

Reads one page of a resource through `getList`. While the next page or filter loads, the rows of the previous one stay on screen.

```typescript
useList<T = any>(input: UseListOptions): UseQueryResult<GetListResult<T>, Error>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `input` | `UseListOptions` | 是 |  |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `enabled` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | 是否允许自动执行查询。 |
| `filters` | `Filter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 筛选条件数组。 |
| `liveMode` | `LiveMode \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to the context's `liveMode`, which is `auto` with a liveProvider. |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `onLiveEvent` | `((event: LiveEvent) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called for each event of the resource, in `auto` and `manual` mode. |
| `pagination` | `false \| Pagination \| undefined` | 否 | `{ page, pageSize }`, pages counted from 1; `false` asks for every record. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `sorters` | `Sorter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 排序条件数组。 |

返回 `UseQueryResult<GetListResult<T>, Error>`。

### useLocale

The current locale, re-rendering when it changes; `en-US` without an i18n provider.

```typescript
useLocale(): string
```

返回 `string`。

### useLogin

Signs in through `authProvider.login`; once it succeeds the previous session's cached data is dropped. Returns a mutation: `mutate({ username, password })`.

```typescript
useLogin(): UseMutationResult<AuthActionResponse, Error, any>
```

返回 `UseMutationResult<AuthActionResponse, Error, any>`。

### useLogout

Signs out through `authProvider.logout` and drops the session's cached data.

```typescript
useLogout(): UseMutationResult<AuthActionResponse, Error, any | undefined>
```

返回 `UseMutationResult<AuthActionResponse, Error, any>`。

### useLumi

The `LumiContext` of the nearest Lumi provider: the data providers, the request client and every other service.

```typescript
useLumi(): LumiContext
```

返回 `LumiContext`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Permission checks. |
| `agentBridge` | `AgentBridge \| undefined` | 否 | What the agent sees of the running pages and the client tools it acts through. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The log of writes. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The sign-in session. |
| `config` | `Readonly<LumiConfig>` | 是 | The config `createLumi` was given. |
| `getDataProvider` | `(name?: string) => DataProvider` | 是 | The data provider registered under `name`, `default` when left out; throws for a name that is not registered. |
| `http` | `HttpClient` | 是 | The request client, signed by `authProvider`. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Translation, the locale and formats. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Changes pushed by the backend. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws notification cards. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation and the current address. |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws toasts. |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | Checks form values against `rules`. |

### useLumiSpec

The Spec rendered by the nearest LumiSpecPage or LumiSpecSource, for components
that show part of the document itself, such as an example's source.

```typescript
useLumiSpec(): Spec | undefined
```

返回 `Spec | undefined`。

### useMany

Reads several records by id: one `getMany` call when the provider has it, else one `getOne` per id.

```typescript
useMany<T = any>(input: UseManyOptions): UseQueryResult<ManyResult<T>, Error>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `input` | `UseManyOptions` | 是 |  |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `enabled` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | 是否允许自动执行查询。 |
| `ids` | `ID[]` | 是 | The records to read. |
| `liveMode` | `LiveMode \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to the context's `liveMode`, which is `auto` with a liveProvider. |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `onLiveEvent` | `((event: LiveEvent) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called for each event of the resource, in `auto` and `manual` mode. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |

返回 `UseQueryResult<ManyResult<T>, Error>`。

### useModal

Opens a modal with content rendered by `render`; the handle's `close(value)` resolves its `result`.

```typescript
useModal(): { open: (options: OverlayOpenOptions) => OverlayHandle; }
```

返回 `{ open: (options: OverlayOpenOptions) => OverlayHandle; }`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `open` | `(options: OverlayOpenOptions) => OverlayHandle` | 是 |  |

### useNavigation

Navigation through the router provider, like the `navigate` action; inert without one.

```typescript
useNavigation(): { go: (config: GoConfig) => void; back: () => void; }
```

返回 `{ go: (config: GoConfig) => void; back: () => void; }`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `back` | `() => void` | 是 |  |
| `go` | `(config: GoConfig) => void` | 是 |  |

### useNotify

Opens notification cards through the configured NotificationProvider, like the `notify` action.

```typescript
useNotify(): FeedbackHandle<NotificationParams>
```

返回 `FeedbackHandle<NotificationParams>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `close` | `(key: string) => void` | 是 | Closes the entry with this key. |
| `open` | `(params: NotificationParams) => string \| undefined` | 是 | Returns the key of the open entry, or `undefined` without a provider. |

### useOne

Reads one record through `getOne`; sends nothing while `id` is undefined.

```typescript
useOne<T = any>(input: UseOneOptions): UseQueryResult<SingleResult<T>, Error>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `input` | `UseOneOptions` | 是 |  |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `enabled` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | 是否允许自动执行查询。 |
| `id` | `ID \| undefined` | 是 | The record to read; nothing is sent while it is undefined. |
| `liveMode` | `LiveMode \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to the context's `liveMode`, which is `auto` with a liveProvider. |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `onLiveEvent` | `((event: LiveEvent) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called for each event of the resource, in `auto` and `manual` mode. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |

返回 `UseQueryResult<SingleResult<T>, Error>`。

### useOptionalTranslate

Like `useTranslate`, for components that may render outside a Lumi provider: there it returns the default message, else the key.

```typescript
useOptionalTranslate(): (key: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, defaultMessage?: string) => string
```

返回 `(key: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, defaultMessage?: string) => string`。

### useOverlay

The overlay controller of the nearest `LumiOverlayProvider`, which `useModal`, `useDrawer` and `useDialog` open through.

```typescript
useOverlay(): OverlayController
```

返回 `OverlayController`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dispose` | `() => void` | 是 | Closes every overlay and drops the listeners. |
| `entries` | `OverlayEntry[]` | 是 | The open overlays, the newest last. |
| `getSnapshot` | `() => OverlayEntry[]` | 是 | The open overlays as an immutable list, for `useSyncExternalStore`. |
| `open` | `(options: OverlayOptions) => OverlayHandle` | 是 | Opens an overlay; the handle closes it and carries its `result`. |
| `registerCloseGuard` | `(id: number, guard: OverlayCloseGuard) => () => void` | 是 | Adds a check that may refuse a close; returns the function removing it. |
| `requestClose` | `(id: number, request?: { reason?: OverlayCloseReason; value?: unknown; }) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Closes an overlay once its close guards agree; resolves whether it closed. |
| `subscribe` | `(listener: () => void) => () => void` | 是 | Notifies when the open overlays change. |

### useOverlayCloseGuard

Registers a check an overlay runs before it closes, e.g. to confirm unsaved changes; returns whether it is inside an overlay.

```typescript
useOverlayCloseGuard(guard: OverlayCloseGuard, active?: boolean): boolean
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `guard` | `OverlayCloseGuard` | 是 |  |
| `active` | `boolean` | 否 |  |

返回 `boolean`。

### useParsedLocation

The current address as the router provider parses it, re-rendering when it changes.

```typescript
useParsedLocation(): ParsedLocation
```

返回 `ParsedLocation`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `action` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The action of the address, e.g. `edit`, when the router knows it. |
| `id` | `ID \| undefined` | 否 | The record of the address, when the router knows it. |
| `params` | `Record<string, unknown>` | 是 | The path's params, e.g. `{ id: "12" }` for `/orders/:id`. |
| `pathname` | `string` | 是 | The path without query or fragment. |
| `query` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | The query parameters. |
| `resource` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The resource the address belongs to, when the router knows it. |

### usePermissions

The raw permission data of `authProvider.getPermissions`. Checks go through `useCan`, which asks the access provider.

```typescript
usePermissions(params?: any): UseQueryResult<unknown, Error>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `params` | `any` | 否 |  |

返回 `UseQueryResult<unknown, Error>`。

### usePublish

Sends an event through the liveProvider, when it can publish.

```typescript
usePublish(): (event: LiveEvent) => Promise<void>
```

返回 `(event: LiveEvent) => Promise<void>`。

### useRegister

Creates an account through `authProvider.register`; when the provider signs it in, cached session data resets as after `login`.

```typescript
useRegister(): UseMutationResult<AuthActionResponse, Error, any>
```

返回 `UseMutationResult<AuthActionResponse, Error, any>`。

### useResourceLive

Keeps a resource's queries live (spread the returned meta into the query): in `auto` mode an event on
`resources/{resource}` refetches them (lists, records and aggregates
together, so a list and its stats stay one snapshot). With `ids`, events
naming other records are ignored.

```typescript
useResourceLive(resource: string, dataProviderName: string | undefined, options?: LiveOptions & { ids?: readonly ID[]; enabled?: boolean; }): LiveQueryMeta
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 是 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `options` | `LiveOptions & { ids?: readonly ID[]; enabled?: boolean; }` | 否 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `enabled` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | 是否允许自动执行查询。 |
| `ids` | `readonly ID[] \| undefined` | 否 | 记录主键数组。 |
| `liveMode` | `LiveMode \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to the context's `liveMode`, which is `auto` with a liveProvider. |
| `onLiveEvent` | `((event: LiveEvent) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called for each event of the resource, in `auto` and `manual` mode. |

返回 `LiveQueryMeta`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `lumiLive` | `LiveMode` | 是 |  |

### useSelect

Options of a remote select: loaded page by page from a resource, searched on the server, narrowed by other fields, with the selected rows fetched by id when they are not on a loaded page.

```typescript
useSelect(input: UseSelectOptions): UseSelectResult
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `input` | `UseSelectOptions` | 是 |  |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `avatarField` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A field holding the option's avatar image. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `debounce` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Milliseconds a typed keyword waits before it is searched; 300 by default. |
| `dependsOn` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Fields of `deps` whose values filter the options with `eq`, e.g. the city list by `province`. |
| `deps` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Current values of the fields in `dependsOn`. |
| `descriptionField` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A field shown as the option's second line. |
| `enabled` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | `false` loads nothing, e.g. until the select opens. |
| `filters` | `Filter[] \| undefined` | 否 | Conditions every request carries. |
| `imageField` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A field holding the option's picture. |
| `labelField` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The field shown; the first of `label`, `name`, `title`, `id` with a value when left out. |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `pageSize` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Options loaded per request. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | The resource the options come from. |
| `search` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A keyword kept by the caller; the options follow it when it changes. |
| `searchField` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The field a search filters with `like`; without it there is no server search. |
| `selected` | `ID \| ID[] \| null \| undefined` | 否 | The chosen value or values; rows not on a loaded page are fetched by id so their labels show. |
| `valueField` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The field used as the value; `id` by default. |

返回 `UseSelectResult`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `hasMore` | `boolean` | 是 | Whether more options remain on the server. |
| `keyword` | `string` | 是 | The keyword being searched. |
| `loading` | `boolean` | 是 | The first page of the current search is pending or loading. |
| `loadingMore` | `boolean` | 是 | A further page is loading. |
| `loadMore` | `() => void` | 是 | Loads the next page. |
| `options` | `Option[]` | 是 | Rows of every loaded page, in server order. |
| `search` | `(keyword: string) => void` | 是 | Searches for a keyword, from the first page. |
| `selectedOptions` | `Option[]` | 是 | The rows behind `selected`, fetched by id when they are not among the loaded pages. |
| `setDeps` | `(values: Record<string, unknown>) => void` | 是 | Replaces the dependency values, reloading the options. |
| `total` | `number \| undefined` | 是 | How many options match the search, when the server says. |

### useSubscription

Listens to a live channel while mounted; inert without a liveProvider.

```typescript
useSubscription(options: UseSubscriptionOptions): void
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `UseSubscriptionOptions` | 是 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `channel` | `string` | 是 | The channel to listen on; a resource's is `resources/{resource}`. |
| `enabled` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | `false` does not subscribe. |
| `onLiveEvent` | `(event: LiveEvent) => void` | 是 | Called for each event. |
| `types` | `readonly string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Only these event types; all of them when left out. |

返回 `void`。

### useTable

The state of a list page: page, page size, sorters, the rows of the current page and an optional aggregate. The JSON `ListPage` is built on it.

```typescript
useTable<T = any>(input: UseTableOptions): UseTableReturn<T>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `input` | `UseTableOptions` | 是 |  |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `aggregate` | `UseTableAggregateOptions \| undefined` | 否 | An endpoint answering totals for the same filters: `{ url, method?, totalKey? }`. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `filters` | `Filter[] \| undefined` | 否 | Conditions of the list; a change returns to the first page. |
| `liveMode` | `LiveMode \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to the context's `liveMode`, which is `auto` with a liveProvider. |
| `meta` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `onLiveEvent` | `((event: LiveEvent) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called for each event of the resource, in `auto` and `manual` mode. |
| `pagination` | `{ pageSize?: number; } \| undefined` | 否 | The first page size, 10 by default; the table keeps page and size in its state from there. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | The resource listed. |
| `statePath` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where page, page size and sorters live in page state, so JSON elements can read them; a private path when left out. |
| `syncWithLocation` | `string \| boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Keeps page, page size and sort in the address's query (`page`, `pageSize`, `sort=field:asc,field2:desc`), so a reload, a shared link and back/forward restore them. A string prefixes the parameter names, for several synced tables on one page. Needs the context's routerProvider. |

返回 `UseTableReturn<T>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `aggregate` | `TableAggregate \| undefined` | 否 | The aggregate's values, once they have loaded for the rows on screen. |
| `aggregateQuery` | `UseQueryResult<{ data: TableAggregate; }, Error> \| undefined` | 否 | The aggregate request, when `aggregate` is set. |
| `page` | `number` | 是 | The current page, counted from 1. |
| `pageSize` | `number` | 是 | Records per page. |
| `query` | `UseQueryResult<GetListResult<T>, Error>` | 是 | The `useList` result behind the rows, for loading and error states. |
| `rows` | `T[]` | 是 | The records of the current page. |
| `setPage` | `Dispatch<SetStateAction<number>>` | 是 | Moves to another page. |
| `setPageSize` | `Dispatch<SetStateAction<number>>` | 是 | Changes the page size. |
| `setSorters` | `Dispatch<SetStateAction<Sorter[]>>` | 是 | Changes the sort. |
| `sorters` | `Sorter[]` | 是 | The current sort, one entry per column. |
| `total` | `number` | 是 | How many records match, across all pages. |

### useTenant

The current tenant and a way to switch it; inert without a `tenantProvider`.

```typescript
useTenant(): UseTenantResult
```

返回 `UseTenantResult`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `current` | `TenantOption \| undefined` | 是 | The option of the current tenant, when the provider lists them. |
| `options` | `readonly TenantOption[]` | 是 | The tenants the reader may switch between. |
| `setTenant` | `(value: string) => void` | 是 | Switches the tenant; every query is then fetched again for it. |
| `tenant` | `string \| undefined` | 是 | The current tenant's value, undefined before one is chosen. |

### useTenantReset

Drops every Lumi query when the tenant changes and fetches the mounted ones
again, as signing in does, so one tenant's cache never shows in another;
the live connection reopens with the new tenant.

```typescript
useTenantReset(client: QueryClient): void
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `client` | `QueryClient` | 是 |  |

返回 `void`。

### useToast

Opens light messages through the configured ToastProvider, like the `toast` action.

```typescript
useToast(): FeedbackHandle<ToastParams>
```

返回 `FeedbackHandle<ToastParams>`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `close` | `(key: string) => void` | 是 | Closes the entry with this key. |
| `open` | `(params: ToastParams) => string \| undefined` | 是 | Returns the key of the open entry, or `undefined` without a provider. |

### useTranslate

The translate function `t(key, params?, defaultMessage?)` of the i18n provider, renewed when the locale changes; without a provider it returns the default message, else the key.

```typescript
useTranslate(): (key: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, defaultMessage?: string) => string
```

返回 `(key: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, defaultMessage?: string) => string`。

### useTreeSource

Remote nodes for a hierarchical control. A full source reads the whole
resource once; a lazy one reads the root level, then each level as its
parent opens. Chosen values are echoed: their rows are read by ID and the
branches leading to them are loaded, so labels and paths resolve.

```typescript
useTreeSource(input: TreeSource, options?: { value?: unknown; }): TreeSourceState
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `input` | `{ resource: string; dataProviderName?: string \| undefined; meta?: Record<string, unknown> \| undefined; filters?: { field: string; operator: "null" \| "in" \| "eq" \| "ne" \| "lt" \| "lte" \| ... 6 more ... \| "nnull"; value: unknown; }[] \| undefined; ... 8 more ...; pageSize?: number \| undefined; }` | 是 |  |
| `options` | `{ value?: unknown; }` | 否 |  |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `childrenField` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The field holding a row's nested children, for a backend that answers a tree. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `filters` | `{ field: string; operator: "null" \| "in" \| "eq" \| "ne" \| "lt" \| "lte" \| "gt" \| "gte" \| "like" \| "nlike" \| "nin" \| "between" \| "nnull"; value: unknown; }[] \| undefined` | 否 | Conditions every request carries. |
| `labelField` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The field shown; the first of `label`, `name`, `title` with a value, else the id, when left out. |
| `lazy` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Requests each level when its parent opens, instead of the whole resource once. |
| `leafField` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A field telling whether a row has no children, so a lazy node shows no expander. |
| `meta` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `pageSize` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Rows per request; 1000 by default. |
| `parentField` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The field linking a flat row to its parent; `parentId` by default. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | The resource the nodes come from. |
| `rootValue` | `string \| number \| undefined` | 否 | The parent value of root rows; rows with an empty parent when left out. |
| `searchField` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The field a search filters with `like` on the server; a lazy source needs it to search. |
| `valueField` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The field used as a node's value; `id` by default. |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `value` | `unknown` | 否 |  |

返回 `TreeSourceState`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `items` | `TreeSourceNode[]` | 是 | The nodes loaded so far, as a tree. |
| `loadChildren` | `((node: { value: unknown; }) => Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Loads one node's children; only present for a `lazy` source. |
| `loading` | `boolean` | 是 | The first level (or the whole resource) is loading. |
| `search` | `((query: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Server search; only present for a `lazy` source with a `searchField`. |
| `searching` | `boolean` | 是 | A server search is under way. |
| `selectedNodes` | `TreeSourceNode[]` | 是 | The chosen nodes, also when their branch has not been loaded yet. |

### useUpdate

Updates a record through `update`, then refetches the resource's queries: `mutate({ id, variables })`.

```typescript
useUpdate<T = any, V = any>(options: MutationOptions<SingleResult<T>, UpdateVariables<V>>): UseMutationResult<SingleResult<T>, Error, UpdateVariables<V>>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `MutationOptions<SingleResult<T>, UpdateVariables<V>>` | 是 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `mutationMode` | `MutationMode \| undefined` | 否 | Updates and deletes only: `optimistic` shows the change at once and rolls it back on failure; `undoable` also waits `undoableTimeout` with an Undo notice before sending (Undo rejects with MutationUndoneError, unreported). Defaults to the context's `mutationMode`, else `pessimistic`. |
| `onError` | `((error: Error, variables: UpdateVariables<V>) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Replaces the default report: without it a failure is shown as a danger toast. |
| `onSuccess` | `((data: SingleResult<T>, variables: UpdateVariables<V>) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Called with the saved data once the write succeeded. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | The resource written to; its lists, records and aggregates refetch after a success. |
| `undoableTimeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Milliseconds an `undoable` write waits for Undo before it is sent; 5000 by default. |

返回 `UseMutationResult<SingleResult<T>, Error, UpdateVariables<V>>`。

### useUpdateMany

Applies the same values to several records: `updateMany` when the provider has it, else one `update` per id.

```typescript
useUpdateMany<T = any, V = any>(options: MutationOptions<ManyResult<T>, UpdateManyVariables<V>>): UseMutationResult<ManyResult<T>, Error, UpdateManyVariables<V>>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `options` | `MutationOptions<ManyResult<T>, UpdateManyVariables<V>>` | 是 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `mutationMode` | `MutationMode \| undefined` | 否 | Updates and deletes only: `optimistic` shows the change at once and rolls it back on failure; `undoable` also waits `undoableTimeout` with an Undo notice before sending (Undo rejects with MutationUndoneError, unreported). Defaults to the context's `mutationMode`, else `pessimistic`. |
| `onError` | `((error: Error, variables: UpdateManyVariables<V>) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Replaces the default report: without it a failure is shown as a danger toast. |
| `onSuccess` | `((data: ManyResult<T>, variables: UpdateManyVariables<V>) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Called with the saved data once the write succeeded. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | The resource written to; its lists, records and aggregates refetch after a success. |
| `undoableTimeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Milliseconds an `undoable` write waits for Undo before it is sent; 5000 by default. |

返回 `UseMutationResult<ManyResult<T>, Error, UpdateManyVariables<V>>`。

### useUpdatePassword

Sets a new password through `authProvider.updatePassword`, e.g. with a reset link's token.

```typescript
useUpdatePassword(): UseMutationResult<AuthActionResponse, Error, any>
```

返回 `UseMutationResult<AuthActionResponse, Error, any>`。

### useUpload

Uploads files with progress and cancellation, through the data provider's client or straight to object storage with `sign`; defaults come from the context's `upload` option.

```typescript
useUpload(input?: UseUploadOptions): UseUploadReturn
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `input` | `UseUploadOptions` | 否 |  |

`input` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `context` | `LumiContext \| undefined` | 否 | The Lumi context to use outside a Lumi provider. |
| `data` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Other form fields sent beside the file. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The data provider whose API root and request client are used. |
| `fieldName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Form field of the file; `file` by default. |
| `headers` | `UploadHeaders \| undefined` | 否 | Extra headers, or a function answering them; the reader's credentials are added by the request client. |
| `method` | `"POST" \| "PUT" \| "PATCH" \| undefined` | 否 | `POST` by default. |
| `sign` | `UploadSignOptions \| undefined` | 否 | Uploads straight to object storage with a ticket from the backend, instead of through `url`. |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | Aborts the upload. |
| `trustedOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Other sites an upload `url` may point at and be signed for; the API root and this site always are. |
| `url` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the file is sent, relative to the API root; `/upload` by default. |
| `withCredentials` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Sends cookies; follows the auth provider's credentials when left out. |

返回 `UseUploadReturn`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `cancel` | `() => void` | 是 | Aborts the upload under way. |
| `error` | `Error \| null` | 是 | Why the last `upload` failed. |
| `progress` | `number` | 是 | Percent of the files `upload` is sending, 0 to 100. |
| `reset` | `() => void` | 是 | Clears the progress and the error. |
| `upload` | `(input: File \| File[] \| FileList, options?: UploadOptions) => Promise<UploadResult[]>` | 是 | Sends the files one after another and answers their results in order. |
| `uploadFile` | `(file: File, options?: UploadOptions & { onProgress?: (percent: number) => void; }) => Promise<UploadResult>` | 是 | Sends one file on its own: several may run at once, each with its own progress and `signal`. It leaves the shared progress/uploading/error alone. |
| `uploading` | `boolean` | 是 | True while `upload` runs; another call is refused until it ends. |

### useValidator

Returns `validate(values)`, which checks any values against `rules` with the context's Validator and answers the translated issues.

```typescript
useValidator(rules?: ValidationRules): (values: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<FormValidationIssue[]>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `rules` | `unknown` | 否 |  |

返回 `(values: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<FormValidationIssue[]>`。

### validateDocument

Checks a page Spec against a Catalog and answers `{ valid, issues }`, each issue at a JSON Pointer path. The runtime loader and `lumi-check` share it.

```typescript
validateDocument(document: unknown, catalog: Catalog, options?: { maxElements?: number; maxDepth?: number; directives?: readonly DirectiveDefinition[]; }): DocumentValidation
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `document` | `unknown` | 是 |  |
| `catalog` | `Catalog<SchemaDefinition<SchemaType<string, unknown>, SchemaType<string, unknown>>, unknown>` | 是 |  |
| `options` | `{ maxElements?: number; maxDepth?: number; directives?: readonly DirectiveDefinition[]; }` | 否 |  |

`options` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `directives` | `readonly DirectiveDefinition<z.ZodType<unknown, unknown, z.core.$ZodTypeInternals<unknown, unknown>>>[] \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `maxDepth` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `maxElements` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |

返回 `DocumentValidation`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `issues` | `DocumentIssue[]` | 是 |  |
| `valid` | `boolean` | 是 |  |

### validateForm

Validates values with a Validator and answers the issues with translated messages.

```typescript
validateForm(validator: Validator, rules: ValidationRules, values: Record<string, unknown>, i18nProvider?: Pick<I18nProvider, "translate">): Promise<FormValidationIssue[]>
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `validator` | `Validator` | 是 | 统一的数据校验器，可接 AJV、Zod、Valibot 或自定义实现。 |
| `rules` | `unknown` | 是 |  |
| `values` | `Record<string, unknown>` | 是 |  |
| `i18nProvider` | `Pick<I18nProvider, "translate"> \| undefined` | 否 | 翻译、切换语言和格式化。 |

`validator` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `validate` | `(rules: ValidationRules, values: Record<string, unknown>) => ValidationIssue[] \| Promise<ValidationIssue[]>` | 是 | The issues of `values` under `rules`, each at a dotted field path; none when they pass. May be asynchronous. |

`i18nProvider` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `translate` | `(key: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, defaultMessage?: string) => string` | 是 | The text for `key` in the current locale, with `{name}` placeholders filled from `params`; `defaultMessage`, else the key, when there is none. |

返回 `Promise<FormValidationIssue[]>`。

### withPageTranslations

The provider a page with its own `translations` renders under. A key is
looked up in the page's table for the active locale, then in `provider`
(the shared translations and Lumi's own, active then fallback locale),
then in the page's table for the fallback locale, then the default.
Everything else — locale, switching, formats — is `provider`'s.

```typescript
withPageTranslations(provider: I18nProvider, translations: I18nTranslations): I18nProvider
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `provider` | `I18nProvider` | 是 |  |
| `translations` | `I18nTranslations` | 是 |  |

`provider` 的字段：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `changeLocale` | `(locale: string, params?: unknown) => Promise<void> \| void` | 是 | Switches the locale. |
| `formatCurrency` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An amount in the configured currency and unit. |
| `formatDate` | `((value: I18nDateValue, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A date in the locale's form. |
| `formatDateTime` | `((value: string, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An ISO 8601 UTC string shown in the configured time zone. |
| `formatNumber` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A number in the locale's form; components use `Intl` without it. |
| `formatRelativeTime` | `((value: number, unit: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatUnit, options?: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A relative time such as "3 days ago". |
| `getDirection` | `(() => LumiDirection) \| undefined` | 否 | The current locale's text direction. |
| `getFallbackLocale` | `(() => string \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | The locale a missing translation falls back to, when there is one. |
| `getFormatConfig` | `(() => I18nFormatConfig) \| undefined` | 否 | The currency and time zone settings. |
| `getLocale` | `() => string` | 是 | The current locale, e.g. `zh-CN`. |
| `subscribe` | `((listener: () => void) => () => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Notifies when the locale changes; with it `$t` and component text follow the switch. |
| `translate` | `(key: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, defaultMessage?: string) => string` | 是 | The text for `key` in the current locale, with `{name}` placeholders filled from `params`; `defaultMessage`, else the key, when there is none. |

返回 `I18nProvider`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `changeLocale` | `(locale: string, params?: unknown) => Promise<void> \| void` | 是 | Switches the locale. |
| `formatCurrency` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An amount in the configured currency and unit. |
| `formatDate` | `((value: I18nDateValue, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A date in the locale's form. |
| `formatDateTime` | `((value: string, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An ISO 8601 UTC string shown in the configured time zone. |
| `formatNumber` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A number in the locale's form; components use `Intl` without it. |
| `formatRelativeTime` | `((value: number, unit: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatUnit, options?: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A relative time such as "3 days ago". |
| `getDirection` | `(() => LumiDirection) \| undefined` | 否 | The current locale's text direction. |
| `getFallbackLocale` | `(() => string \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | The locale a missing translation falls back to, when there is one. |
| `getFormatConfig` | `(() => I18nFormatConfig) \| undefined` | 否 | The currency and time zone settings. |
| `getLocale` | `() => string` | 是 | The current locale, e.g. `zh-CN`. |
| `subscribe` | `((listener: () => void) => () => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Notifies when the locale changes; with it `$t` and component text follow the switch. |
| `translate` | `(key: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, defaultMessage?: string) => string` | 是 | The text for `key` in the current locale, with `{name}` placeholders filled from `params`; `defaultMessage`, else the key, when there is none. |

## 类

### AgentRequestError

A failed agent request; `status` lets the auth provider react to 401 and 403.

```typescript
new AgentRequestError(status: number, message: string, data?: unknown): AgentRequestError
```

| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `status` | `number` | 是 |  |
| `message` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `data` | `unknown` | 否 |  |

返回 `AgentRequestError`。

返回结构：

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `cause` | `unknown` | 否 |  |
| `data` | `unknown` | 是 |  |
| `message` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `name` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `stack` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `status` | `number` | 是 |  |

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `cause` | `unknown` | 否 |  |
| `data` | `unknown` | 是 |  |
| `message` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `name` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `stack` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

| `status` | `number` | 是 |  |

### translateDirectiveSchema

`{ "$t": "Revenue" }` reads a message from the active locale, falling back to
`default` and then to the key itself, so a Spec written in one language
renders unchanged where no table covers it. `params` fill `{name}`
placeholders and may be any prop expression, e.g. `{ "$state": "/count" }`.

```typescript
const translateDirectiveSchema: z.ZodObject<{ $t: z.ZodString; params: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodUnknown>>; default: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>; }, z.core.$strict>
```

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi/catalog`

Where a hierarchical control (Tree, TreeSelect, Cascader, CascaderPanel)
reads its nodes from: one DataProvider resource. Rows are either nested
under `childrenField` or flat and linked by `parentField`. With `lazy`,
each level is requested when its parent opens, filtered by
`parentField = <parent value>`; root rows have an empty parent unless
`rootValue` names one.

```typescript
const treeSourceSchema: z.ZodObject<{ resource: z.ZodString; dataProviderName: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>; meta: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodUnknown>>; filters: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodArray<z.ZodObject<{ field: z.ZodString; operator: z.ZodEnum<...>; value: z.ZodUnknown; }, z.core.$strip>>>; ... 8 more ...; pageSize: z.ZodOption...
```

### uploadRequestSchema

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi/catalog`

```typescript
const uploadRequestSchema: z.ZodObject<{ sign: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodObject<{ url: z.ZodString; data: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodUnknown>>; complete: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>; }, z.core.$strict>>; ... 4 more ...; dataProviderName: z.ZodOptional<...>; }, z.core.$strict>
```

### writeParamsSchema

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi/catalog`

Update and delete also choose when the change shows; forms always wait for the server.

```typescript
const writeParamsSchema: z.ZodObject<{ resource: z.ZodString; dataProviderName: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>; meta: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodRecord<z.ZodString, z.ZodUnknown>>; id: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodUnion<readonly [z.ZodString, z.ZodNumber]>>; values: z.ZodOptional<...>; resultPath: z.ZodOptional<...>; mutationMode: z.ZodOptional<...>; undoableTim...
```

```typescript

```

### AgentBridge

```typescript
interface AgentBridge
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `getApprovalMode` | `() => AgentApprovalMode` | 是 |  |
| `onReference` | `(listener: (items: AgentChatContextItem[]) => void) => () => void` | 是 |  |
| `reference` | `(items: AgentChatContextItem[]) => void` | 是 | Puts records into the conversation, e.g. selected table rows: the open conversation adds them to the next message's context. Kept until one listens. |
| `registerNavigation` | `(navigation: AgentNavigation) => () => void` | 是 |  |
| `registerPage` | `(page: AgentPageHandle) => () => void` | 是 |  |
| `setApprovalMode` | `(mode: AgentApprovalMode) => void` | 是 |  |
| `snapshot` | `() => AgentUiSnapshot` | 是 |  |
| `subscribe` | `(listener: () => void) => () => void` | 是 | Called when the approval mode or the registered pages change. |
| `toolDefinitions` | `() => AgentToolDefinition[]` | 是 |  |
| `tools` | `() => Record<string, AgentClientTool>` | 是 |  |

### AgentBridgeOptions

```typescript
interface AgentBridgeOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `approvalMode` | `AgentApprovalMode \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `navigation` | `(() => AgentNavigation \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | Navigation used until a host registers its own, e.g. the Lumi router provider. |
| `safeActions` | `readonly string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Actions an event may run without asking; others change data and ask in `ask` mode. |
| `storageKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the approval mode is kept between visits; leave out to keep it in memory. |
| `translate` | `((key: string, fallback: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | Resolves `{ $t }` labels and the approval copy. |

### AgentChat

```typescript
interface AgentChat
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `answer` | `(questionId: string, answer: string \| string[]) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Answers a question the agent asked. |
| `approve` | `(approvalId: string, approved: boolean, reason?: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Answers an approval request, accepting or refusing it. |
| `edit` | `(messageId: string, text: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Rewrites a user message and answers from there. |
| `error` | `string \| null` | 是 | Why the last reply failed. |
| `id` | `string` | 是 | The conversation's id. |
| `loading` | `boolean` | 是 | True while the conversation's earlier messages load. |
| `messages` | `AgentChatMessage[]` | 是 | The conversation so far. |
| `reset` | `(next?: { id?: string \| null; messages?: AgentChatMessage[]; }) => void` | 是 | Starts a new, empty conversation. |
| `retry` | `() => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Asks again for an answer to the last user message. |
| `send` | `(input: AgentSendInput) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Sends a message; ignored while a reply is under way or when it is empty. |
| `session` | `AgentChatSession` | 是 | The session behind the chat, for calls the hook does not surface. |
| `skip` | `(questionId: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 | Skips a question the agent asked. |
| `status` | `AgentChatStatus` | 是 | `ready`, `submitted` while a reply is requested, `streaming` while it arrives, or `error`. |
| `stop` | `() => void` | 是 | Stops the reply under way, keeping what arrived. |
| `streaming` | `boolean` | 是 | True while a reply is requested or streaming. |

| `skip` | `(questionId: string) => Promise<boolean>` | 是 |  |
| `stop` | `() => void` | 是 |  |
| `subscribe` | `(listener: () => void) => () => void` | 是 |  |

| `transport` | `AgentTransport` | 是 |  |

### AgentMergeState

What a merge keeps between chunks besides the message itself.

```typescript
interface AgentMergeState
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |

| `message` | `AgentChatMessage` | 是 |  |
| `started` | `Record<string, number>` | 是 | When each reasoning block and tool call started, for its duration. |


| --- | --- | --- | --- |

### AuthProvider

Authentication provider: the one sign-in session of the app. It runs the
session requests itself (sign in and out, register, password reset, the
current user), decides how every other request carries the credentials
(`authorizeRequest`) and what a failure means (`onError`). Data providers
never handle credentials: they send through the HttpClient that asks it.

```typescript
interface AuthProvider
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `authorizeRequest` | `((request: { url: string; method: string; }) => RequestAuthorization \| undefined \| Promise<RequestAuthorization \| undefined>) \| undefined` | 否 | Headers and fetch credentials for one request; called before each one an HttpClient sends. |
| `check` | `() => Promise<CheckResponse>` | 是 | Whether a session exists; asked before a protected route or manifest loads. |
| `forgotPassword` | `((params: any) => Promise<AuthActionResponse>) \| undefined` | 否 | Starts a password reset, e.g. by mailing a link to the account's address. |
| `getIdentity` | `(<T = any>() => Promise<T \| null>) \| undefined` | 否 | The current user, for the account menu and `useIdentity`; `null` when signed out. |
| `getPermissions` | `((params?: any) => Promise<unknown>) \| undefined` | 否 | The raw permission data, for `usePermissions`. Lumi's own checks ask the AccessProvider. |
| `login` | `(params: any) => Promise<AuthActionResponse>` | 是 | Signs in with the sign-in form's values, e.g. `{ username, password, remember }`. |
| `logout` | `(params?: any) => Promise<AuthActionResponse>` | 是 | Signs out and forgets the session. |
| `onError` | `((error: any) => Promise<AuthErrorResponse>) \| undefined` | 否 | What a failed data request means for the session, once `refresh` could not save it; `{ logout: true }` signs the reader out. |
| `refresh` | `(() => Promise<boolean>) \| undefined` | 否 | Renews expired credentials after a 401; `true` sends the request again. Concurrent 401s share one call. |
| `register` | `((params: any) => Promise<AuthActionResponse>) \| undefined` | 否 | Creates an account; a provider that also signs the new account in answers like `login`. Leave it out where accounts are assigned. |
| `updatePassword` | `((params: any) => Promise<AuthActionResponse>) \| undefined` | 否 | Sets a new password, with the reset link's token or the signed-in session. |

### DataProvider

Data + request provider (Lumi REST conventions, framework-agnostic).
All data access goes through this interface; it can be swapped for any REST / GraphQL / Mock implementation.

```typescript
interface DataProvider
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `create` | `<T = any, V = any>(params: CreateParams<V>) => Promise<SingleResult<T>>` | 是 | Creates a record from `variables` and answers the saved one. |
| `createMany` | `(<T = any, V = any>(params: CreateManyParams<V>) => Promise<ManyResult<T>>) \| undefined` | 否 | Creates several records in one request; without it they are created one by one. |
| `custom` | `(<T = any>(params: CustomParams) => Promise<SingleResult<T>>) \| undefined` | 否 | A request outside the resource conventions; the `request` action, `useCustom` and `useCustomMutation` need it. |
| `deleteMany` | `(<T = any, V = any>(params: DeleteManyParams<V>) => Promise<ManyResult<T>>) \| undefined` | 否 | Deletes several records; without it they are deleted one by one. |
| `deleteOne` | `<T = any, V = any>(params: DeleteParams<V>) => Promise<SingleResult<T>>` | 是 | Deletes the record `id`. |
| `getApiUrl` | `(() => string) \| undefined` | 否 | The API root, for callers that build full addresses such as uploads. |
| `getList` | `<T = any>(params: GetListParams) => Promise<GetListResult<T>>` | 是 | One page of a resource, filtered and sorted, or all of it with `pagination: false`; `total` counts every match. |
| `getMany` | `(<T = any>(params: GetManyParams) => Promise<ManyResult<T>>) \| undefined` | 否 | Several records by id in one request; without it each id is read with `getOne`. |
| `getOne` | `<T = any>(params: GetOneParams) => Promise<SingleResult<T>>` | 是 | One record by id. |
| `http` | `HttpClient \| undefined` | 否 | The client it sends through; uploads and the agent reuse its credentials. |
| `update` | `<T = any, V = any>(params: UpdateParams<V>) => Promise<SingleResult<T>>` | 是 | Updates the record `id` with `variables` and answers the saved one. |
| `updateMany` | `(<T = any, V = any>(params: UpdateManyParams<V>) => Promise<ManyResult<T>>) \| undefined` | 否 | Applies the same `variables` to several records; without it they are updated one by one. |

### HttpAgentTransportOptions

```typescript
interface HttpAgentTransportOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `body` | `Record<string, unknown> \| (() => Record<string, unknown>) \| undefined` | 否 | Extra fields sent with every request, e.g. the model. |

| `fetch` | `((input: RequestInfo \| URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `headers` | `AgentHeaders \| undefined` | 否 | Extra headers; the auth provider's credentials come through `http`. |
| `http` | `HttpClient \| undefined` | 否 | Sends through this client, so the auth provider signs the request and a 401 refreshes once; without one the request goes out unsigned. |
| `prepareBody` | `((request: AgentRequest, body: Record<string, unknown>) => Record<string, unknown>) \| undefined` | 否 | Replaces the default request body `{ id, messages, trigger, messageId, ...body }`, e.g. to send only the last message to a server that keeps the history. |

### HttpClient

```typescript
interface HttpClient
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `baseUrl` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `download` | `(path: string, options?: HttpRequestOptions) => Promise<Response>` | 是 | The same request, for an answer that is a file: the response itself on 2xx, its body unread so the caller can follow the stream, else a `DataProviderError`. |
| `extend` | `(options: HttpClientExtendOptions) => HttpClient` | 是 |  |
| `fetch` | `(path: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>` | 是 | A signed fetch: on a 401 the auth's `refresh` runs once (shared by every request waiting on it) and the request is sent again. The response is returned as it is, whatever its status. |
| `getAuthProvider` | `() => RequestSigner \| undefined` | 是 | The provider signing these requests, if any. |
| `own` | `<E>(error: E) => E` | 是 | Marks a failure as this client's, so only a signed client's reaches the auth's `onError`. |
| `prepare` | `(request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => Promise<HttpPreparedRequest>` | 是 | The request as this client sends it: credentials, locale, tenant, then `onRequest`. For transports `fetch` does not cover, such as an XHR upload, which hands the answer to `receive`. |
| `receive` | `(response: Response, request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => Promise<Response>` | 是 | Runs `onResponse` on an answer to a prepared request. |
| `request` | `<T = any>(path: string, options?: HttpRequestOptions) => Promise<T>` | 是 | A signed JSON request; the parsed body on 2xx, else a `DataProviderError`. |
| `signed` | `boolean` | 是 | Whether the app's auth signs these requests and handles their failures. |
| `url` | `(path: string) => string` | 是 | The path joined onto `baseUrl`. |

### HttpClientOptions

```typescript
interface HttpClientOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `auth` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | `false` sends no app credentials and leaves failures to the caller: a 401 from such a backend does not sign the reader out. Its own key, if any, goes in `headers`. Defaults to `true`. |
| `authProvider` | `RequestSigner \| (() => RequestSigner \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | The app's auth provider (or just its signing part); a function is read on every request. |
| `baseUrl` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Root that relative paths join onto; absolute URLs pass through. |
| `fetcher` | `((input: RequestInfo \| URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>) \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to the global fetch, read when a request starts. |
| `getLocale` | `(() => string \| null \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | Current UI locale, sent as `Accept-Language`. |
| `getTenant` | `(() => string \| null \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | Current tenant, sent as `tenantHeader`. |
| `headers` | `HttpHeaders \| undefined` | 否 | Headers on every request, below the per-request and auth ones; a function is read on every request. |
| `onRequest` | `((request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => HttpOutgoingRequest \| void \| Promise<HttpOutgoingRequest \| void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Last look at a request before it leaves, after the credentials, locale, tenant and JSON encoding: add or rewrite headers, sign the method, URL and body. Runs again for the resend after a 401 refresh. Change the request in place or return a new one. |
| `onResponse` | `((response: Response, request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => Response \| void \| Promise<Response \| void>) \| undefined` | 否 | First look at the final answer, whatever its status: check its signature, decrypt it, or reshape it. Return another Response to replace it; throw to fail the request. |
| `parseError` | `((body: unknown, response: Response) => HttpErrorDetails) \| undefined` | 否 | Reads this backend's error body (parsed JSON, or `{}` when it is not JSON) into the one `DataProviderError` every caller handles. Defaults to `parseLumiError`. |
| `tenantHeader` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to `X-Tenant`. |
| `trustedOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Other sites that get the same treatment as `baseUrl` and the page, e.g. `["https://files.example.com"]`. A request to any other site goes out plain: without the reader's credentials, the tenant, the default headers or the hooks, and its 401 does not sign the reader out. |

### HttpErrorDetails

What `parseError` reads out of a failed answer; anything left out falls back to the status.

```typescript
interface HttpErrorDetails
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `code` | `unknown` | 否 |  |
| `data` | `unknown` | 否 |  |
| `errors` | `HttpFieldError[] \| undefined` | 否 | Field errors; the form puts each under its field. |
| `message` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |

### HttpFieldError

One field's server error; forms show `message` under the field at `path`, dotted for nested fields, e.g. `items.0.sku`.

```typescript
interface HttpFieldError
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `code` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `message` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `path` | `string` | 是 |  |

| `headers` | `Record<string, string>` | 是 | 请求头。 |
| `method` | `string` | 是 | HTTP 方法。 |
| `url` | `string` | 是 | 请求地址。 |

| `credentials` | `RequestCredentials \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `headers` | `Record<string, string>` | 是 | 请求头。 |
| `method` | `string` | 是 | HTTP 方法。 |
| `url` | `string` | 是 | 请求地址。 |

```typescript
interface HttpRequestOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `headers` | `Record<string, string> \| undefined` | 否 | 请求头。 |

### I18nProvider

Translation, the current locale and locale-aware formats. `$t` in page JSON
and every text a component ships go through it; `translate`, `changeLocale`
and `getLocale` are required, the rest refine what components show.

```typescript
interface I18nProvider
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `changeLocale` | `(locale: string, params?: unknown) => Promise<void> \| void` | 是 | Switches the locale. |
| `formatCurrency` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An amount in the configured currency and unit. |
| `formatDate` | `((value: I18nDateValue, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A date in the locale's form. |
| `formatDateTime` | `((value: string, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | An ISO 8601 UTC string shown in the configured time zone. |
| `formatNumber` | `((value: number, options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A number in the locale's form; components use `Intl` without it. |
| `formatRelativeTime` | `((value: number, unit: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatUnit, options?: Intl.RelativeTimeFormatOptions, locale?: string) => string) \| undefined` | 否 | A relative time such as "3 days ago". |
| `getDirection` | `(() => LumiDirection) \| undefined` | 否 | The current locale's text direction. |
| `getFallbackLocale` | `(() => string \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | The locale a missing translation falls back to, when there is one. |
| `getFormatConfig` | `(() => I18nFormatConfig) \| undefined` | 否 | The currency and time zone settings. |
| `getLocale` | `() => string` | 是 | The current locale, e.g. `zh-CN`. |
| `subscribe` | `((listener: () => void) => () => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Notifies when the locale changes; with it `$t` and component text follow the switch. |
| `translate` | `(key: string, params?: Record<string, unknown>, defaultMessage?: string) => string` | 是 | The text for `key` in the current locale, with `{name}` placeholders filled from `params`; `defaultMessage`, else the key, when there is none. |

| `processed` | `number` | 是 |  |
| `succeeded` | `number` | 是 |  |

### LiveEvent

Realtime changes pushed by the backend. A resource's changes arrive on its
channel, `resources/{resource}`, so lists and records refetch when another
reader (or a job, or an agent's backend tool) changes them.

```typescript
interface LiveEvent
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `channel` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `date` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `payload` | `{ [key: string]: unknown; ids?: ID[]; } \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `type` | `string` | 是 | `created`, `updated`, `deleted`, or a type of the backend's own. |

### LiveOptions

```typescript
interface LiveOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `liveMode` | `LiveMode \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to the context's `liveMode`, which is `auto` with a liveProvider. |
| `onLiveEvent` | `((event: LiveEvent) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called for each event of the resource, in `auto` and `manual` mode. |

### LiveProvider

Receives the changes the backend pushes. Lumi subscribes each mounted list
and record to its resource's channel and refetches what an event touches.

```typescript
interface LiveProvider
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `publish` | `((event: LiveEvent) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Sends an event to the backend, for providers that can. |
| `reconnect` | `(() => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Opens the connection again, e.g. with the new tenant's headers; called when the tenant changes. |
| `subscribe` | `(params: LiveSubscribeParams) => () => void` | 是 | Returns the function that ends the subscription. |

### LiveQueryMeta

Query `meta` that tells an `auto` reader's refetch which queries opted out.

```typescript
interface LiveQueryMeta
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `lumiLive` | `LiveMode` | 是 |  |

### LiveSubscribeParams

One subscription: a channel, optionally narrowed to some event types.

```typescript
interface LiveSubscribeParams
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `channel` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `onLiveEvent` | `(event: LiveEvent) => void` | 是 |  |
| `types` | `readonly string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Only these event types; all of them when left out. |


| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `action` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | 否 |  |
| `fallback` | `ReactNode` | 否 | Shown when access is refused; nothing shows while it is checked. |
| `params` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |

### LumiConfig

```typescript
interface LumiConfig
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accessProvider` | `AccessProvider \| undefined` | 否 | 权限判断。 |
| `agent` | `AgentConfig \| undefined` | 否 | Agent chat requests: the default endpoint, the data source and extra headers. |
| `auditLogProvider` | `AuditLogProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Records each accepted write and lists the record history; optional. |
| `authProvider` | `AuthProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The app's one sign-in session; it also signs every request `http` sends. |
| `dataProvider` | `DataProvider \| Record<string, DataProvider> \| undefined` | 否 | Pass one for a single data source; pass \{ default: ..., other: ... \} for multiple |
| `env` | `Record<string, string \| number \| boolean \| null> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `http` | `HttpClient \| undefined` | 否 | The client uploads and the agent send through when their data provider has none. Defaults to one signed by `authProvider` that sends the locale and tenant; data providers built on it share those credentials. |
| `i18nProvider` | `I18nProvider \| undefined` | 否 | 翻译、切换语言和格式化。 |
| `liveMode` | `LiveMode \| undefined` | 否 | The default for every query; `auto` when a liveProvider is set. |
| `liveProvider` | `LiveProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Pushes backend changes; with it, queries refetch what an event touches. |
| `mutationMode` | `MutationMode \| undefined` | 否 | The default for updates and deletes; `pessimistic` waits for the server. Forms always wait. |
| `notificationProvider` | `NotificationProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws `notify` notices; without one the action is a no-op. |
| `routerProvider` | `RouterProvider \| undefined` | 否 | 与框架无关的导航。 |
| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The tenant (workspace) requests belong to; queries refetch when it changes. |
| `toastProvider` | `ToastProvider \| undefined` | 否 | Draws `toast` feedback and automatic error reports; without one they are no-ops. |
| `undoableTimeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Milliseconds an undoable write waits for Undo; defaults to 5000. |
| `upload` | `UploadOptions \| undefined` | 否 | 全局上传地址、请求头和允许的目标来源。 |
| `validator` | `Validator \| undefined` | 否 | 统一的数据校验器，可接 AJV、Zod、Valibot 或自定义实现。 |

| `tenantProvider` | `TenantProvider \| undefined` | 否 | The current tenant. |

### MutationOptions

```typescript
interface MutationOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `mutationMode` | `MutationMode \| undefined` | 否 | Updates and deletes only: `optimistic` shows the change at once and rolls it back on failure; `undoable` also waits `undoableTimeout` with an Undo notice before sending (Undo rejects with MutationUndoneError, unreported). Defaults to the context's `mutationMode`, else `pessimistic`. |
| `onError` | `((error: Error, variables: TVariables) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Replaces the default report: without it a failure is shown as a danger toast. |
| `onSuccess` | `((data: TData, variables: TVariables) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Called with the saved data once the write succeeded. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | The resource written to; its lists, records and aggregates refetch after a success. |
| `undoableTimeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Milliseconds an `undoable` write waits for Undo before it is sent; 5000 by default. |

### PackedDocument

Packed documents: a page, manifest or theme sent as `{ "packed": "…" }`
instead of readable JSON, so its text does not show in a browser's network
panel. The value is the JSON text, raw-deflated, masked with a key when one
is set, and written as base64.

This hides a document from a casual look, nothing more: the browser unpacks
it, so a reader who steps through the page still reaches it. What a reader
must not learn has to stay on the server.

```typescript
interface PackedDocument
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `packed` | `string` | 是 |  |

### RunExportOptions

```typescript
interface RunExportOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `columns` | `readonly TransferColumn[] \| undefined` | 否 | The columns of the file, in order; every field of the records when left out. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `filename` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Name of the saved file without its extension; the resource name by default. |
| `filters` | `Filter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 筛选条件数组。 |
| `format` | `(string & {}) \| TransferFormat \| undefined` | 否 | `csv` or `json` for a file written here; with `url`, any name the backend knows (`xlsx`…). |
| `maxItems` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Stops after this many records, so an export cannot run away. |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `method` | `"GET" \| "POST" \| undefined` | 否 | `POST` (default) sends the values as JSON; `GET` as the query, `filters`, `sorters` and `columns` as JSON text. |
| `onProgress` | `((progress: { loaded: number; total?: number; }) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `pageSize` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Records asked for per request. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | 用于取消请求的 AbortSignal。 |
| `sorters` | `Sorter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 排序条件数组。 |
| `url` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The backend builds the file: this endpoint (relative to the API root) is sent `{ filters, sorters, columns, format, ...meta }` and answers the file as a stream, in any format. Without it the records are loaded page by page and the file is written in the browser. |

### RunImportOptions

```typescript
interface RunImportOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `batchSize` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Records sent per `createMany` call, for backends whose bulk create takes or refuses a batch as a whole. Without it each record goes through `create`, so every refused row is reported on its own. |
| `columns` | `readonly TransferColumn[] \| undefined` | 否 | Maps the file's headings to record fields; headings are taken as field names when left out. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `onProgress` | `((progress: ImportProgress) => void) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | 用于取消请求的 AbortSignal。 |
| `url` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The backend performs the import: the chosen file is uploaded to this endpoint (relative to the API root) as the form field `file`, with `meta` beside it, and the answer `{ data: { total, succeeded, failed, errors } }` is the result. Without it the file is read here and each record created. |

### SimpleAuthEndpoints

```typescript
interface SimpleAuthEndpoints
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `forgotPassword` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | POST with `{ email }` to start a password reset. Leave it out to offer none. |
| `login` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | POST with the login form; answers `{ data: { token?, user, permissions } }` (no token in cookie mode). |
| `logout` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | POST signed with the session; failures are ignored. |
| `me` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | GET signed with the session; answers `{ data: { user, permissions } }`. |
| `refresh` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | POST after a 401 to renew the session; answers `{ data: { token } }` in bearer mode, sets the cookie in cookie mode. Leave it out to sign the reader out on the first 401. |
| `register` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | POST with the sign-up form. Answering like `login` signs the new account in; answering without a session sends the reader to sign in. Leave it out where accounts are assigned, and no sign-up is offered. |
| `updatePassword` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | POST with `{ token, password }` from the reset link. |

### SimpleAuthProviderOptions

```typescript
interface SimpleAuthProviderOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `apiUrl` | `string` | 是 | API root the endpoints are relative to. |
| `csrf` | `SimpleAuthCsrf \| undefined` | 否 | Cookie mode: the anti-forgery cookie echoed in a header on writes. |
| `endpoints` | `SimpleAuthEndpoints \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `http` | `HttpClient \| undefined` | 否 | The client its own requests go through (for the locale, tenant or a test fetcher); one is created otherwise. |
| `loginPath` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where a signed-out reader is sent. |
| `mode` | `"bearer" \| "cookie" \| undefined` | 否 | `bearer` (default) keeps the token login returns and sends it as `Authorization: Bearer …`. `cookie` keeps nothing: the server's session cookie travels with `credentials: 'include'` and `me` tells who is in. |
| `permission` | `SimplePermissionFormat \| undefined` | 否 | How a `{ resource, action }` check reads the permission strings login returns. |
| `readCookie` | `((name: string) => string \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | Cookie mode: where `csrf.cookie` is read; defaults to `document.cookie`. |
| `storage` | `TokenStorage \| undefined` | 否 | Bearer mode: defaults to `globalThis.localStorage`; pass one for SSR or tests. |
| `tokenKey` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Bearer mode: storage key for the token. |

### SimpleAuthSession

```typescript
interface SimpleAuthSession
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `permissions` | `string[]` | 是 |  |
| `user` | `User \| null` | 是 |  |

### SimpleDataProviderOptions

```typescript
interface SimpleDataProviderOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `apiUrl` | `string` | 是 | API root URL, e.g. https://api.example.com |
| `http` | `HttpClient \| undefined` | 否 | The client requests go through, carrying the auth provider's credentials, the locale and the tenant. Without one requests carry none of them. |
| `onRequest` | `((request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => HttpOutgoingRequest \| void \| Promise<HttpOutgoingRequest \| void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Changes every request to this backend just before it leaves: extra or rewritten headers, a signature over the method, URL and body. See `HttpClientOptions.onRequest`. |
| `onResponse` | `((response: Response, request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => Response \| void \| Promise<Response \| void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Checks or rewrites every answer from this backend, e.g. verifies its signature. |
| `parseError` | `((body: unknown, response: Response) => HttpErrorDetails) \| undefined` | 否 | Reads a failed answer's body into `{ message, code?, errors?: [{ path, message }] }`; defaults to the Lumi convention. Field errors appear under the form's fields. |
| `unwrap` | `((body: any) => { data: any; total?: number; raw?: unknown; }) \| undefined` | 否 | Custom response unwrapping; defaults to the Lumi convention: data = body.data ?? body, total = body.meta.total |

### SimplePermissionFormat

Permission strings in the backend's own shape. A check builds
`format` (`{resource}{separator}{action}` by default), with each `/` of the
resource replaced by the separator: `system/users` + `delete` is
`system:users:delete`, or `system.users.delete` with `.`. A granted segment
`*` matches any one segment, and a trailing `*` everything below it, so
`system:*` covers `system:users:delete` and `*` grants all.

```typescript
interface SimplePermissionFormat
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `format` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | With `{resource}`, `{action}` and `{separator}`; `{action}{separator}{resource}` reads `delete:system:users`. |
| `separator` | `":" \| "." \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to `:`. |

### SingleResult

```typescript
interface SingleResult
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `data` | `T` | 是 | The record. |
| `raw` | `unknown` | 否 | The backend's whole answer. |

```typescript
interface Sorter
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `field` | `string` | 是 |  |
| `order` | `SortOrder` | 是 |  |

### SseLiveProvider

```typescript

```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `close` | `() => void` | 是 |  |
| `publish` | `((event: LiveEvent) => void \| Promise<void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Sends an event to the backend, for providers that can. |
| `reconnect` | `() => void` | 是 | Closes and opens the stream again, e.g. once the tenant or the token changed. |
| `subscribe` | `(params: LiveSubscribeParams) => () => void` | 是 | Returns the function that ends the subscription. |

### SseLiveProviderOptions

```typescript
interface SseLiveProviderOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `credentials` | `RequestCredentials \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `fetcher` | `((input: RequestInfo \| URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>) \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `headers` | `(() => Record<string, string>) \| undefined` | 否 | Extra headers for each connection, e.g. the token and the tenant; read again on every reconnect. |
| `http` | `HttpClient \| undefined` | 否 | Opens and posts through this client, so the auth provider signs each connection (read again on every reconnect) with the locale and tenant. |
| `publishUrl` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where `publish` posts an event; without it the provider only listens. |
| `retryDelay` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | First wait before reconnecting, doubled up to 30 seconds. |
| `url` | `string` | 是 | The event stream endpoint; it sends `data: { channel, type, payload }` frames. |

### TenantOption

The tenant (workspace, team, store) the reader works in. Data requests
carry it, and every cached query is dropped and fetched again when it
changes, so one tenant's data never shows in another. Framework-free: the
React root subscribes to it.

```typescript
interface TenantOption
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `description` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A second line in the switcher. |
| `label` | `string` | 是 | The name shown. |
| `logo` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Image address shown beside the name. |
| `value` | `string` | 是 | What requests carry, e.g. in `X-Tenant`. |

### TenantProvider

Holds the current tenant. The request client reads it for every request,
and Lumi listens to it to drop cached data when it changes.

```typescript
interface TenantProvider
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `get` | `() => string \| undefined` | 是 | The current tenant's value, or undefined before one is chosen. |
| `options` | `(() => readonly TenantOption[]) \| undefined` | 否 | The tenants the reader may switch between, when the provider knows them. |
| `set` | `(value: string) => void` | 是 | Switches to another tenant. |
| `subscribe` | `(listener: () => void) => () => void` | 是 | Notifies when the tenant changes; Lumi drops its cached data on it. |

### ToastParams

A light, single-line message: saves, copies and every automatic error report.
