| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `duration` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Milliseconds before it closes; `0` keeps it until dismissed. |
| `key` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Replaces an open toast with the same key instead of stacking a copy. |
| `message` | `string` | 是 | The one line shown. |
| `tone` | `FeedbackTone \| undefined` | 否 | Semantic colour. |

### ToastProvider

Draws toasts. Lumi calls it from the `toast` action, `useToast()` and its
automatic error reports; the UI layer supplies the drawing (Lumi UI ships a
toaster), so Lumi stays free of any component library.

```typescript
interface ToastProvider
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `close` | `((key: string) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Closes the toast with this key. |
| `open` | `(params: ToastParams) => string` | 是 | Shows a toast and answers its key. |

### UploadOptions

```typescript
interface UploadOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `data` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Other form fields sent beside the file. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The data provider whose API root and request client are used. |
| `fieldName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Form field of the file; `file` by default. |
| `headers` | `UploadHeaders \| undefined` | 否 | Extra headers, or a function answering them; the reader's credentials are added by the request client. |
| `method` | `"POST" \| "PUT" \| "PATCH" \| undefined` | 否 | `POST` by default. |
| `sign` | `UploadSignOptions \| undefined` | 否 | Uploads straight to object storage with a ticket from the backend, instead of through `url`. |
| `signal` | `AbortSignal \| undefined` | 否 | Aborts the upload. |
| `trustedOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Other sites an upload `url` may point at and be signed for; the API root and this site always are. |
| `url` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the file is sent, relative to the API root; `/upload` by default. |
| `withCredentials` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Sends cookies; follows the auth provider's credentials when left out. |

### UploadResult

```typescript
interface UploadResult
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `name` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The file's name; the local file's when the backend gives none. |
| `raw` | `unknown` | 否 | The backend's whole answer. |
| `size` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Size in bytes. |
| `type` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | MIME type. |
| `url` | `string` | 是 | Where the stored file is read from. |

### UploadSignOptions

Direct-to-storage upload (S3, OSS, COS, R2, MinIO…): the file never passes
through the backend. The backend is asked for a ticket, the file goes to
the address the ticket names, and the backend may be told when it is there.

```typescript
interface UploadSignOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `complete` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Called once the storage accepted the file, relative to the API root: sent `{ name, size, type, ...ticket.complete }`, and what it answers (`{ data: { url, name?, size?, type? } }`) is the upload's result. |
| `data` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Extra values of the ticket request, e.g. `{ "folder": "avatars" }`. |
| `url` | `string` | 是 | Where the ticket is asked for, relative to the API root. Sent `{ name, size, type, ...data }` as JSON; answers an `UploadTicket` (in `data`). |

### UploadTicket

What the backend answers a ticket request with.

```typescript
interface UploadTicket
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `complete` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Values handed back to the `complete` request, e.g. the object key. |
| `fieldName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Form field of the file in a `POST` upload; defaults to `file`. |
| `fields` | `Record<string, string> \| undefined` | 否 | Form fields of a `POST` upload (key, policy, signature…), sent before the file. |
| `fileUrl` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The address the file will be read from; needed unless `complete` answers it. |
| `headers` | `Record<string, string> \| undefined` | 否 | Headers the storage expects, e.g. `Content-Type` or `x-amz-acl`; they must match what was signed. |
| `method` | `"POST" \| "PUT" \| undefined` | 否 | `PUT` sends the file as the body (presigned URL); `POST` sends a form (policy upload). Defaults to `POST` with `fields`, else `PUT`. |
| `url` | `string` | 是 | Where the file goes: a presigned URL, or the bucket's form endpoint. |

### UseAgentChatOptions

```typescript
interface UseAgentChatOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `body` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Extra fields sent with every request, e.g. the model. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The data provider whose API root and request client the chat uses. |
| `endpoint` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to the configured `agent.endpoint`, then `/agent/chat`. |
| `headers` | `AgentHeaders \| undefined` | 否 | Extra request headers; the reader's credentials are added by the request client. |
| `onData` | `((chunk: AgentChunk) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called for each `data-*` chunk of the stream. |
| `onError` | `((error: Error) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called when a reply fails. |
| `onFinish` | `((event: AgentFinishEvent) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called when a reply ends, however it ended. |
| `prepareBody` | `((request: AgentRequest, body: Record<string, unknown>) => Record<string, unknown>) \| undefined` | 否 | Last change to the request body before it is sent. |
| `resource` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A DataProvider resource whose `getOne` returns a conversation's messages; refreshed after each reply. |
| `sessionId` | `string \| null \| undefined` | 否 | The conversation to open; a change switches to it, empty starts a new one. |
| `transport` | `AgentTransport \| undefined` | 否 | Replaces the HTTP transport, e.g. for a WebSocket or an in-page agent. |
| `ui` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Lets the agent see and act on the front end through the Lumi agent bridge: each request carries the UI snapshot (`ui`), the client tools (`clientTools`) and the approval mode (`approvalMode`), and calls to those tools run here. Defaults to true. |

### UseExportOptions

```typescript
interface UseExportOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `columns` | `readonly TransferColumn[] \| undefined` | 否 | The columns of the file, in order; every field of the records when left out. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `filename` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Name of the saved file without its extension; the resource name by default. |
| `filters` | `Filter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 筛选条件数组。 |
| `format` | `(string & {}) \| TransferFormat \| undefined` | 否 | `csv` or `json` for a file written here; with `url`, any name the backend knows (`xlsx`…). |
| `maxItems` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Stops after this many records, so an export cannot run away. |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `method` | `"GET" \| "POST" \| undefined` | 否 | `POST` (default) sends the values as JSON; `GET` as the query, `filters`, `sorters` and `columns` as JSON text. |
| `pageSize` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Records asked for per request. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `save` | `((file: TransferFile \| TransferDownload) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Where the file goes; the browser download by default. |
| `sorters` | `Sorter[] \| undefined` | 否 | 排序条件数组。 |
| `url` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The backend builds the file: this endpoint (relative to the API root) is sent `{ filters, sorters, columns, format, ...meta }` and answers the file as a stream, in any format. Without it the records are loaded page by page and the file is written in the browser. |

### UseExportReturn

```typescript
interface UseExportReturn
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `error` | `Error \| undefined` | 否 | Why the last export failed. |
| `isLoading` | `boolean` | 是 | True while the export runs. |
| `progress` | `{ loaded: number; total?: number; } \| undefined` | 否 | Records loaded so far and their total; with `url`, bytes received and the file's size when the backend sends `Content-Length`. |
| `trigger` | `() => Promise<TransferFile \| TransferDownload \| undefined>` | 是 | Loads the records and saves the file; the newest call wins over an earlier one still loading. |

| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `draft` | `FormDraftOptions<T> \| undefined` | 否 | Keeps unsaved values in the browser so they can be restored later. |
| `groups` | `Record<string, FormGroupDefinition> \| undefined` | 否 | Named groups of fields, such as the steps of a wizard; `groupErrors` reports each group's errors. |
| `id` | `ID \| undefined` | 否 | With an id the record is loaded and the form updates it; without one it creates. |
| `initialValues` | `Partial<T> \| undefined` | 否 | Values of a new record. An edited record initializes once it has loaded, and later refetches leave the edits alone. |
| `meta` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | The resource the form saves to. |
| `rules` | `unknown` | 否 | Business rules for the context's Validator, e.g. a JSON Schema. Leave it out when no validator is configured. |
| `valuesPath` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Where the values live in page state; generated when left out. JSON elements bound to the same path edit the same form. |

### UseFormReturn

```typescript
interface UseFormReturn
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `draft` | `FormDraftControl \| undefined` | 否 | Draft controls, present when the `draft` option is set. |
| `errors` | `FormValidationIssue[]` | 是 | Every current error, the server's field errors included. |
| `firstError` | `(fields?: readonly string[]) => string \| undefined` | 是 | The path of the first field in error, among `fields` when given. |
| `groupErrors` | `Record<string, FormErrorGroup>` | 是 | The errors of each group in `groups`, with their count and first field. |
| `isDirty` | `boolean` | 是 | Whether the values differ from those loaded or last saved. |
| `isSubmitting` | `boolean` | 是 | True while a save is under way; another `submit` then sends nothing. |
| `reset` | `(next?: Partial<T>) => void` | 是 | Returns to the initial values, or to `next`. |
| `setValue` | `(name: keyof T \| string, value: unknown) => void` | 是 | Sets one field; `name` may be a dotted path such as `items.0.sku`. |
| `setValues` | `(next: T \| ((current: T) => T)) => void` | 是 | Replaces every value, or derives them from the current ones. |
| `submit` | `() => Promise<SingleResult<T>>` | 是 | Validates, then saves; rejects when validation fails or the server refuses, with the errors already in `errors`. |
| `validate` | `(options?: FormValidateOptions) => Promise<FormValidationIssue[]>` | 是 | Validates all fields, or only `fields`, without saving; a wizard calls it on "Next". |
| `values` | `T` | 是 | The current values. |
| `valuesPath` | `string` | 是 | The page state path the values live at. |

### UseImportOptions

```typescript
interface UseImportOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `accept` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | File types the picker offers, e.g. `.csv,.json`. |
| `batchSize` | `number \| undefined` | 否 | Records sent per `createMany` call, for backends whose bulk create takes or refuses a batch as a whole. Without it each record goes through `create`, so every refused row is reported on its own. |
| `columns` | `readonly TransferColumn[] \| undefined` | 否 | Maps the file's headings to record fields; headings are taken as field names when left out. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `onFinish` | `((result: ImportResult) => void) \| undefined` | 否 | Called with the result once the import ended. |
| `pick` | `((accept?: string) => Promise<Pick<TransferFile, "name" \| "content"> \| undefined>) \| undefined` | 否 | Chooses and reads the file of a browser import; the browser's picker by default. |
| `pickFile` | `((accept?: string) => Promise<File \| undefined>) \| undefined` | 否 | Chooses the file of a backend import (`url`); the browser's picker by default. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `url` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The backend performs the import: the chosen file is uploaded to this endpoint (relative to the API root) as the form field `file`, with `meta` beside it, and the answer `{ data: { total, succeeded, failed, errors } }` is the result. Without it the file is read here and each record created. |

```typescript
interface UseImportReturn
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `cancel` | `() => void` | 是 | Stops after the batch under way. |
| `error` | `Error \| undefined` | 否 | Why the last import failed as a whole. |
| `isLoading` | `boolean` | 是 | True while the import runs. |
| `progress` | `ImportProgress \| undefined` | 否 | Records processed, accepted and refused so far; with `url`, the percent of the file uploaded. |
| `result` | `ImportResult \| undefined` | 否 | How the last import went: totals and the refused rows. |
| `trigger` | `(file?: Pick<TransferFile, "name" \| "content"> \| File) => Promise<ImportResult \| undefined>` | 是 | Opens the file picker, or imports the given file; resolves undefined when the reader cancels. |

A string prefixes the parameter names, for several synced tables on one page. Needs the context's routerProvider. |


| `uploadFile` | `(file: File, options?: UploadOptions & { onProgress?: (percent: number) => void; }) => Promise<UploadResult>` | 是 | Sends one file on its own: several may run at once, each with its own progress and `signal`. It leaves the shared progress/uploading/error alone. |
| `uploading` | `boolean` | 是 | True while `upload` runs; another call is refused until it ends. |

### Validator

Business validation of a form's values. `rules` is whatever the page gives
(`rules` of `submitForm` or `useForm`); the validator decides how to read
them, e.g. as a JSON Schema. Without one configured, rules are refused.

```typescript
interface Validator
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `validate` | `(rules: ValidationRules, values: Record<string, unknown>) => ValidationIssue[] \| Promise<ValidationIssue[]>` | 是 | The issues of `values` under `rules`, each at a dotted field path; none when they pass. May be asynchronous. |

### WriteOptions

```typescript
interface WriteOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `ids` | `readonly ID[] \| undefined` | 否 | The records written; known for updates and deletes. |
| `meta` | `Meta \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `mutationMode` | `MutationMode \| undefined` | 否 | Falls back to `config.mutationMode`; only updates and deletes with ids use it. |
| `operation` | `WriteOperation` | 是 |  |
| `request` | `() => Promise<T>` | 是 | Sends the write to the DataProvider. |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `undoableTimeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `values` | `unknown` | 否 | The values sent, recorded in the audit log and patched into the cache. |

## 类型

### ActionState

```typescript
type ActionState = ActionState
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `get` | `(path: string) => unknown` | 是 | Read a value by JSON Pointer path. |
| `getSnapshot` | `() => StateModel` | 是 | Return the full state object (used by `useSyncExternalStore`). |
| `set` | `(path: string, value: unknown) => void` | 是 | Write a value by JSON Pointer path and notify subscribers. Equality is checked by reference (`===`), not deep comparison. Callers must pass a new object/array reference for changes to be detected. |
| `update` | `(updates: Record<string, unknown>) => void` | 是 | Write multiple values at once and notify subscribers (single notification). Each value is compared by reference (`===`); only paths whose value actually changed are applied. |

### AgentChatStatus

`ready` waits for the reader, `submitted` waits for the first chunk,
`streaming` receives the answer, `error` keeps the last failure until the
next request.

```typescript
type AgentChatStatus = AgentChatStatus
```

### AgentChatToolStatus

The agent message contract shared with Lumi UI's ChatThread. Lumi keeps its
own structural copy so the services never depend on the UI package; parts
the stream does not build are passed through as they are.

```typescript
type AgentChatToolStatus = AgentChatToolStatus
```

### AgentChunk

The chunks of the AI SDK UI message stream protocol (v1) that a Lumi agent
session understands. Servers stream them as Server-Sent Events, one JSON
object per `data:` line, ending with `data: [DONE]`, and mark the response
with the `x-vercel-ai-ui-message-stream: v1` header.

```typescript
type AgentChunk = AgentChunk
```

### AgentHeaders

```typescript
type AgentHeaders = AgentHeaders
```

### AgentRequestTrigger

Why a request is sent: names follow the AI SDK chat request where one exists.

```typescript
type AgentRequestTrigger = AgentRequestTrigger
```

### AgentSessionComponentProps

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi/catalog`

```typescript
type AgentSessionComponentProps = { statePath: string; endpoint?: string | undefined; sessionId?: string | null | undefined; resource?: string | undefined; dataProviderName?: string | undefined; body?: Record<string, unknown> | undefined; }
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `body` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | Extra fields sent with every request, e.g. the model. |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `endpoint` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The chat endpoint; defaults to the configured `agent.endpoint`, then `/agent/chat`. Relative paths resolve against the DataProvider API URL. |
| `resource` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A DataProvider resource, e.g. `agent-sessions`: its `getOne` loads a conversation's messages and it is refreshed after each reply. |
| `sessionId` | `string \| null \| undefined` | 否 | The conversation to open; empty starts a new one with a generated id. |
| `statePath` | `string` | 是 | The state path the session projects `{ id, messages, status, error, streaming }` to; actions name it as `session`. |

### AuthAccountAction

The account flows an auth provider supports beyond signing in and out.

```typescript
type AuthAccountAction = AuthAccountAction
```

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `params` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `resultPath` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Writes `{ can, reason, loading }` here, so other elements can bind `visible` or `disabled` to it. |

### ComposedDefinitions

导入路径：`@duxweb/lumi/catalog`

### HttpClientExtendOptions

What `extend` may change: the root, whether and by whom requests are signed, the default headers, the fetch, the error format.

```typescript
type HttpClientExtendOptions = HttpClientExtendOptions
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `auth` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | `false` sends no app credentials and leaves failures to the caller: a 401 from such a backend does not sign the reader out. Its own key, if any, goes in `headers`. Defaults to `true`. |
| `authProvider` | `RequestSigner \| (() => RequestSigner \| undefined) \| undefined` | 否 | The app's auth provider (or just its signing part); a function is read on every request. |
| `baseUrl` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | Root that relative paths join onto; absolute URLs pass through. |
| `fetcher` | `((input: RequestInfo \| URL, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>) \| undefined` | 否 | Defaults to the global fetch, read when a request starts. |
| `headers` | `HttpHeaders \| undefined` | 否 | Headers on every request, below the per-request and auth ones; a function is read on every request. |
| `onRequest` | `((request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => HttpOutgoingRequest \| void \| Promise<HttpOutgoingRequest \| void>) \| undefined` | 否 | Last look at a request before it leaves, after the credentials, locale, tenant and JSON encoding: add or rewrite headers, sign the method, URL and body. Runs again for the resend after a 401 refresh. Change the request in place or return a new one. |
| `onResponse` | `((response: Response, request: HttpOutgoingRequest) => Response \| void \| Promise<Response \| void>) \| undefined` | 否 | First look at the final answer, whatever its status: check its signature, decrypt it, or reshape it. Return another Response to replace it; throw to fail the request. |
| `parseError` | `((body: unknown, response: Response) => HttpErrorDetails) \| undefined` | 否 | Reads this backend's error body (parsed JSON, or `{}` when it is not JSON) into the one `DataProviderError` every caller handles. Defaults to `parseLumiError`. |
| `trustedOrigins` | `string[] \| undefined` | 否 | Other sites that get the same treatment as `baseUrl` and the page, e.g. `["https://files.example.com"]`. A request to any other site goes out plain: without the reader's credentials, the tenant, the default headers or the hooks, and its 401 does not sign the reader out. |

### LiveMode

`auto` refetches what an event touches, `manual` only calls `onLiveEvent`,
`off` does not subscribe. An `auto` reader's refetch leaves the queries of
`manual` and `off` readers on the same resource alone.

```typescript
type LiveMode = LiveMode
```

### MutationMode

When a write shows on screen. `pessimistic` waits for the server;
`optimistic` changes the cached lists and records at once and puts them
back if the server refuses; `undoable` does the same and waits
`undoableTimeout` before sending, offering Undo on a notice meanwhile.

| `lazy` | `boolean \| undefined` | 否 | Requests each level when its parent opens, instead of the whole resource once. |
| `leafField` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | A field telling whether a row has no children, so a lazy node shows no expander. |

| `resource` | `string` | 是 | The resource the nodes come from. |
| `rootValue` | `string \| number \| undefined` | 否 | The parent value of root rows; rows with an empty parent when left out. |
| `searchField` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | The field a search filters with `like` on the server; a lazy source needs it to search. |

```typescript
type WriteParams = { resource: string; dataProviderName?: string | undefined; meta?: Record<string, unknown> | undefined; id?: string | number | undefined; values?: Record<string, unknown> | undefined; resultPath?: string | undefined; mutationMode?: "pessimistic" | ... 2 more ... | undefined; undoableTimeout?: number | undefined; }
```

| 名称 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dataProviderName` | `string \| undefined` | 否 | 多数据源名称；省略时使用 default。 |
| `id` | `string \| number \| undefined` | 否 | 记录主键。 |
| `meta` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 | 原样传给 DataProvider 的扩展数据。 |
| `mutationMode` | `"pessimistic" \| "optimistic" \| "undoable" \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `resource` | `string` | 是 | 交给 DataProvider 解释的数据目标，如 `orders`。 |
| `resultPath` | `string \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `undoableTimeout` | `number \| undefined` | 否 |  |
| `values` | `Record<string, unknown> \| undefined` | 否 |  |
